    47	
    48	
    49	class LedgerEvent(BaseModel):
    50	    """One immutable ledger entry. event_hash = SHA256(prev_event_hash || canonical event body)."""
    51	
    52	    run_id: str
    53	    sequence: int
    54	    event_id: str
    55	    event_type: EventType
    56	    causation_id: Optional[str] = None
    57	    correlation_id: str
    58	    timestamp: datetime
    59	    payload: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    60	    prev_event_hash: str
    61	    event_hash: str
    62	
    63	
    64	class ArtifactDigest(BaseModel):
    65	    artifact_id: str
    66	    sha256: str
    67	
    68	
    69	class ExecutionRecord(BaseModel):
    70	    """What ran, where, under which isolation, on which inputs, producing which outputs.
    71	
    72	    External tools are brokered (bubblewrap); in-process parsers record backend "in_process" with
    73	    isolation NONE and the SHA-256 of the tool's source module as the executable fingerprint.
    74	    """
    75	
    76	    execution_id: str
    77	    run_id: str
    78	    tool_call_id: Optional[str] = None
    79	    tool_id: str
    80	    tool_version: str
    81	    executable_sha256: Optional[str] = None
    82	    requested_isolation: str
    83	    effective_isolation: str
    84	    network_policy: str
    85	    backend: str
    86	    status: str
    87	    exit_code: Optional[int] = None
    88	    started_at: Optional[str] = None
    89	    finished_at: Optional[str] = None
    90	    input_artifacts: List[ArtifactDigest] = Field(default_factory=list)
    91	    output_artifacts: List[ArtifactDigest] = Field(default_factory=list)
    92	"""
    93	Hash chain primitives and the independent chain verifier.
    94	
    95	event_hash = SHA256(prev_event_hash || canonical_json(event body)), where the body is every event
    96	field except event_hash itself. The first event of a run links to GENESIS_HASH.
    97	"""
    98	
    99	from __future__ import annotations
   100	import hashlib
   101	import json
   102	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   103	from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List
   104	
   105	from ledger.models import LedgerEvent
   106	
   107	GENESIS_HASH = "0" * 64
   108	
   109	
   110	def canonical_json(value: Any) -> str:
   111	    """Deterministic JSON: sorted keys, no whitespace, UTF-8. Non-JSON values raise TypeError."""
   112	    return json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
   113	
   114	
   115	def event_body(event: LedgerEvent) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   116	    return {
   117	        "run_id": event.run_id,
   118	        "sequence": event.sequence,
   119	        "event_id": event.event_id,
   120	        "event_type": event.event_type.value,
   121	        "causation_id": event.causation_id,
   122	        "correlation_id": event.correlation_id,
   123	        "timestamp": event.timestamp.isoformat(),
   124	        "payload": event.payload,
   125	        "prev_event_hash": event.prev_event_hash,
   126	    }
   127	
   128	
   129	def compute_event_hash(prev_event_hash: str, body: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   130	    return hashlib.sha256((prev_event_hash + canonical_json(body)).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   131	
   132	
   133	@dataclass
   134	class LedgerVerification:
   135	    valid: bool
   136	    event_count: int
   137	    head_hash: str
   138	    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
   139	
   140	
   141	def verify_chain(events: Iterable[LedgerEvent]) -> LedgerVerification:
   142	    """Walk a run's events in order and check sequence continuity, hash linkage and every event hash."""
   143	    errors: List[str] = []
   144	    prev_hash = GENESIS_HASH
   145	    count = 0
   146	    run_ids = set()
   147	    for expected_seq, event in enumerate(events, start=1):
   148	        count += 1
   149	        run_ids.add(event.run_id)
   150	        if event.sequence != expected_seq:
   151	            errors.append(f"sequence gap: expected {expected_seq}, found {event.sequence} ({event.event_id})")
   152	        if event.prev_event_hash != prev_hash:
   153	            errors.append(f"broken link at sequence {event.sequence}: prev_event_hash does not match the previous event")
   154	        recomputed = compute_event_hash(event.prev_event_hash, event_body(event))
   155	        if recomputed != event.event_hash:
   156	            errors.append(f"hash mismatch at sequence {event.sequence}: event content was altered")
   157	        prev_hash = event.event_hash
   158	    if len(run_ids) > 1:
   159	        errors.append(f"events from multiple runs in one chain: {sorted(run_ids)}")
   160	    return LedgerVerification(valid=not errors, event_count=count, head_hash=prev_hash, errors=errors)
   161	"""
   162	Append-only ledger storage. There is no update or delete API: events are only appended and read.
   163	
   164	PostgresLedgerStore is canonical; it serializes appends per run with a transaction-scoped advisory
   165	lock, and the database rejects UPDATE/DELETE on ledger_events with a trigger. MemoryLedgerStore is
   166	the explicit ephemeral mode for offline tests.
   167	"""
   168	
   169	from __future__ import annotations
   170	import asyncio
   171	import json
   172	import uuid
   173	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
   174	from datetime import datetime, timezone
   175	from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional
   176	
   177	from ledger.chain import GENESIS_HASH, LedgerVerification, canonical_json, compute_event_hash, event_body, verify_chain
   178	from ledger.models import EventType, LedgerEvent
   179	
   180	
   181	def _new_event(
   182	    run_id: str,
   183	    sequence: int,
   184	    prev_hash: str,
   185	    event_type: EventType,
   186	    payload: Dict[str, Any],
   187	    causation_id: Optional[str],
   188	    correlation_id: str,
   189	) -> LedgerEvent:
   190	    # Round-trip through canonical JSON: payloads must be plain JSON, and the stored form is exact.
   191	    payload = json.loads(canonical_json(payload))
   192	    event = LedgerEvent(
   193	        run_id=run_id,
   194	        sequence=sequence,
   195	        event_id=f"evt_{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
   196	        event_type=event_type,
   197	        causation_id=causation_id,
   198	        correlation_id=correlation_id,
   199	        timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
   200	        payload=payload,
   201	        prev_event_hash=prev_hash,
   202	        event_hash="",
   203	    )
   204	    event.event_hash = compute_event_hash(prev_hash, event_body(event))
   205	    return event
   206	
   207	
   208	class LedgerStore(ABC):
   209	    @abstractmethod
   210	    async def append(
   211	        self,
   212	        run_id: str,
   213	        event_type: EventType,
   214	        payload: Dict[str, Any],
   215	        causation_id: Optional[str],
   216	        correlation_id: str,
   217	    ) -> LedgerEvent:
   218	        ...
   219	
   220	    @abstractmethod
   221	    async def list_events(self, run_id: str) -> List[LedgerEvent]:
   222	        ...
   223	
   224	
   225	class MemoryLedgerStore(LedgerStore):
   226	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   227	        self._events: Dict[str, List[LedgerEvent]] = {}
   228	        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
   229	
   230	    async def append(self, run_id, event_type, payload, causation_id, correlation_id) -> LedgerEvent:
   231	        async with self._lock:
   232	            chain = self._events.setdefault(run_id, [])
   233	            prev = chain[-1].event_hash if chain else GENESIS_HASH
   234	            event = _new_event(run_id, len(chain) + 1, prev, event_type, payload, causation_id, correlation_id)
   235	            chain.append(event)
   236	            return event.model_copy(deep=True)
   237	
   238	    async def list_events(self, run_id: str) -> List[LedgerEvent]:
   239	        return [e.model_copy(deep=True) for e in self._events.get(run_id, [])]
   240	
   241	
   242	class PostgresLedgerStore(LedgerStore):
   243	    async def append(self, run_id, event_type, payload, causation_id, correlation_id) -> LedgerEvent:
   244	        from sqlalchemy import select, text
   245	        from persistence.database import get_session
   246	        from persistence.models import LedgerEventModel
   247	
   248	        async with get_session() as session:
   249	            # Serialize appends for this run: sequence and prev_event_hash are read and written atomically.
   250	            await session.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(:run_id))"), {"run_id": run_id})
   251	            last = (await session.execute(
   252	                select(LedgerEventModel)
   253	                .where(LedgerEventModel.run_id == run_id)
   254	                .order_by(LedgerEventModel.sequence.desc())
   255	                .limit(1)
   256	            )).scalar_one_or_none()
   257	            sequence = (last.sequence + 1) if last else 1
   258	            prev = last.event_hash if last else GENESIS_HASH
   259	            event = _new_event(run_id, sequence, prev, event_type, payload, causation_id, correlation_id)
   260	            session.add(LedgerEventModel(
   261	                event_id=event.event_id,
   262	                run_id=event.run_id,
   263	                sequence=event.sequence,
   264	                event_type=event.event_type.value,
   265	                causation_id=event.causation_id,
   266	                correlation_id=event.correlation_id,
   267	                event_timestamp=event.timestamp.isoformat(),
   268	                payload_json=canonical_json(event.payload),
   269	                prev_event_hash=event.prev_event_hash,
   270	                event_hash=event.event_hash,
   271	            ))
   272	            await session.flush()
   273	            return event
   274	
   275	    async def list_events(self, run_id: str) -> List[LedgerEvent]:
   276	        from sqlalchemy import select
   277	        from persistence.database import get_session
   278	        from persistence.models import LedgerEventModel
   279	
   280	        async with get_session() as session:
   281	            rows = (await session.execute(
   282	                select(LedgerEventModel)
   283	                .where(LedgerEventModel.run_id == run_id)
   284	                .order_by(LedgerEventModel.sequence)
   285	            )).scalars().all()
   286	            return [
   287	                LedgerEvent(
   288	                    run_id=r.run_id,
   289	                    sequence=r.sequence,
   290	                    event_id=r.event_id,
   291	                    event_type=EventType(r.event_type),
   292	                    causation_id=r.causation_id,
   293	                    correlation_id=r.correlation_id,
   294	                    timestamp=datetime.fromisoformat(r.event_timestamp),
   295	                    payload=json.loads(r.payload_json),
   296	                    prev_event_hash=r.prev_event_hash,
   297	                    event_hash=r.event_hash,
   298	                )
   299	                for r in rows
   300	            ]
   301	
   302	
   303	class InvestigationLedger:
   304	    """Append-only investigation ledger over a LedgerStore."""
   305	
   306	    def __init__(self, store: Optional[LedgerStore] = None) -> None:
   307	        self.store: LedgerStore = store or MemoryLedgerStore()
   308	
   309	    async def append(
   310	        self,
   311	        run_id: str,
   312	        event_type: EventType,
   313	        payload: Dict[str, Any],
   314	        causation_id: Optional[str] = None,
   315	        correlation_id: Optional[str] = None,
   316	    ) -> LedgerEvent:
   317	        return await self.store.append(run_id, EventType(event_type), payload, causation_id, correlation_id or run_id)
   318	
   319	    async def events(self, run_id: str) -> List[LedgerEvent]:
   320	        return await self.store.list_events(run_id)
   321	
   322	    async def verify(self, run_id: str) -> LedgerVerification:
   323	        return verify_chain(await self.events(run_id))
   324	"""
   325	Run-bound ledger recorder used by the investigation controller.
   326	
   327	Every method appends one typed event. Evidence is admitted only with full provenance (source
   328	artifact, source execution, source tool call) unless it is explicitly baseline evidence.
   329	"""
   330	
   331	from __future__ import annotations
   332	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
   333	
   334	from domain.evidence import Evidence
   335	from ledger.models import EventType, ExecutionRecord, LedgerEvent
   336	from ledger.store import InvestigationLedger
   337	
   338	PREVIEW_CHARS = 2000
   339	
   340	
   341	class LedgerProvenanceError(ValueError):
   342	    """Evidence without source artifact / execution / tool call references cannot enter the ledger."""
   343	
   344	
   345	def _clip(text: Optional[str], limit: int = PREVIEW_CHARS) -> Optional[str]:
   346	    if text is None:
   347	        return None
   348	    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[:limit] + "... [truncated]"
   349	
   350	
   351	def evidence_payload(ev: Evidence) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   352	    data = ev.model_dump(mode="json", exclude={"raw_reference", "scan_id"})
   353	    data["description"] = _clip(data.get("description"))
   354	    return data
   355	
   356	
   357	class RunRecorder:
   358	    def __init__(self, ledger: InvestigationLedger, run_id: str) -> None:
   359	        self.ledger = ledger
   360	        self.run_id = run_id
   361	
   362	    async def _append(self, event_type: EventType, payload: Dict[str, Any], causation_id: Optional[str] = None) -> LedgerEvent:
   363	        return await self.ledger.append(self.run_id, event_type, payload, causation_id=causation_id)
   364	
   365	    async def run_started(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> LedgerEvent:
   366	        return await self._append(EventType.RUN_STARTED, payload)
   367	
   368	    async def artifact_ingested(self, artifact: Any, causation_id: str) -> LedgerEvent:
   369	        return await self._append(EventType.ARTIFACT_INGESTED, _artifact_payload(artifact), causation_id)
   370	
   371	    async def model_called(self, payload: Dict[str, Any], causation_id: Optional[str]) -> LedgerEvent:
   372	        payload = dict(payload)
   373	        payload["content"] = _clip(payload.get("content"))
   374	        payload["reasoning_content"] = _clip(payload.get("reasoning_content"))
   375	        return await self._append(EventType.MODEL_CALLED, payload, causation_id)
   376	
   377	    async def tool_called(self, call_id: str, tool_name: str, arguments: Dict[str, Any], causation_id: str) -> LedgerEvent:
   378	        return await self._append(EventType.TOOL_CALLED, {"tool_call_id": call_id, "tool": tool_name, "arguments": arguments}, causation_id)
   379	
   380	    async def execution_completed(self, record: ExecutionRecord, summary: str, causation_id: str) -> LedgerEvent:
   381	        payload = record.model_dump(mode="json")
   382	        payload["summary"] = _clip(summary, 600)
   383	        return await self._append(EventType.EXECUTION_COMPLETED, payload, causation_id)
   384	
   385	    async def artifact_created(self, artifact: Any, causation_id: str) -> LedgerEvent:
   386	        return await self._append(EventType.ARTIFACT_CREATED, _artifact_payload(artifact), causation_id)
   387	
   388	    async def evidence_asserted(self, ev: Evidence, causation_id: Optional[str], baseline: bool = False) -> LedgerEvent:
   389	        if not baseline:
   390	            missing = [f for f in ("source_artifact_id", "source_execution_id", "source_tool_call_id") if not getattr(ev, f)]
   391	            if missing:
   392	                raise LedgerProvenanceError(f"Evidence {ev.id} ({ev.type}) lacks provenance: {', '.join(missing)}")
   393	        payload = evidence_payload(ev)
   394	        payload["baseline"] = baseline
   395	        return await self._append(EventType.EVIDENCE_ASSERTED, payload, causation_id)
   396	
   397	    async def hypothesis_proposed(self, hyp: Any, causation_id: Optional[str]) -> LedgerEvent:
   398	        return await self._append(EventType.HYPOTHESIS_PROPOSED, {
   399	            "hypothesis_id": hyp.id, "claim_type": hyp.claim_type, "claim_summary": hyp.claim_summary,
   400	            "target": hyp.target_identifier, "status": hyp.epistemic_status.value,
   401	        }, causation_id)
   402	
   403	    async def hypothesis_transitioned(self, payload: Dict[str, Any], causation_id: Optional[str]) -> LedgerEvent:
   404	        return await self._append(EventType.HYPOTHESIS_TRANSITIONED, payload, causation_id)
   405	
   406	    async def supervisor_decision(self, decision_type: str, action: str, details: Dict[str, Any],
   407	                                  causation_id: Optional[str]) -> LedgerEvent:
   408	        return await self._append(EventType.SUPERVISOR_DECISION,
   409	                                  {"decision_type": decision_type, "action": action, "details": details}, causation_id)
   410	
   411	    async def run_finalized(self, payload: Dict[str, Any], causation_id: Optional[str]) -> LedgerEvent:
   412	        return await self._append(EventType.RUN_FINALIZED, payload, causation_id)
   413	
   414	
   415	def _artifact_payload(artifact: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   416	    return {
   417	        "artifact_id": artifact.artifact_id,
   418	        "handle": artifact.handle,
   419	        "sha256": artifact.sha256,
   420	        "size_bytes": artifact.size_bytes,
   421	        "artifact_type": artifact.artifact_type,
   422	        "mime_type": artifact.mime_type,
   423	        "storage_uri": artifact.storage_uri,
   424	        "parent_artifact_ids": list(artifact.parent_artifact_ids),
   425	        "produced_by_execution_id": artifact.produced_by_execution_id,
   426	        "produced_by_tool_call_id": artifact.produced_by_tool_call_id,
   427	        "producer": artifact.producer,
   428	        "producer_version": artifact.producer_version,
   429	    }

--- [Step 16 | THINKING] ---
I'm finding real gaps in the ledger design: there's no check preventing events from being appended after RUN_FINALIZED, and a race could even produce two finalization records with ambiguous resolution. Also the hash chain just uses plain SHA256 without a key, so anyone with database write access could rewrite the entire chain and recompute matching hashes, since there's no signing or external anchoring of the chain head to catch tampering.

I'm also noticing the row-level DELETE trigger likely won't fire on TRUNCATE, meaning tail events could be wiped undetectably unless there's a statement-level BEFORE TRUNCATE trigger too. On top of that, the evidence provenance check only verifies fields are non-empty rather than confirming source_execution_id, source_artifact_id, or tool_call_id actually correspond to real records, making these references forgeable — and baseline evidence bypasses provenance entirely since it comes from an in-process triage run with no execution record, yet still factors into risk scoring.

Looking at the hashing logic, canonical JSON with ensure_ascii=False should handle floats and control characters fine, but a payload containing lone surrogates (e.g., from surrogateescape-decoded hostile bytes) would survive json.dumps only to crash on the subsequent utf-8 encode in compute_event_hash, causing the whole append — and run — to fail unless sanitization strips those first. I want to check the persistence models and migrations next to see how triggers and unique constraints on (run_id, sequence) are actually defined.

   182	        poolclass=pool.NullPool,

   233	def include_object(object: Any, name: Optional[str], type_: str, reflected: bool, compare_to: Any) -> bool:
   234	    if type_ == "table" and reflected and compare_to is None and name in UNMANAGED_LEGACY_TABLES:
   235	        return False
   236	    return True
   237	"""
   238	Database engine and session management for KAVACH AI 2.0.
   239	Uses SQLAlchemy 2.x async engine with Psycopg 3 (postgresql+psycopg://).
   240	"""
   241	
   242	from __future__ import annotations
   243	import os
   244	import logging
   245	from typing import AsyncGenerator
   246	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
   247	
   248	from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
   249	    create_async_engine,
   250	    async_sessionmaker,
   251	    AsyncSession,
   252	    AsyncEngine,
   253	)
   254	
   255	logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.persistence")

   257	from pathlib import Path
   258	from urllib.parse import quote_plus
   259	from dotenv import load_dotenv

   263	if _env_path.exists():

   265	
   266	_engine: AsyncEngine | None = None
   267	_session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession] | None = None
   268	
   269	
   270	def _postgres_password() -> str:
   271	    """Read the password from POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE (Docker/Compose secret) or POSTGRES_PASSWORD.
   272	
   273	    There is no default password: a missing credential fails closed.
   274	    """
   275	    password_file = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE")
   276	    if password_file:
   277	        return Path(password_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
   278	    password = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD")
   279	    if password:
   280	        return password.strip()
   281	    raise RuntimeError("PostgreSQL password not configured: set POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE or POSTGRES_PASSWORD")
   282	
   283	
   284	def get_postgres_url() -> str:
   285	    """Build the psycopg 3 PostgreSQL URL (used by both the async engine and Alembic)."""
   286	    host = os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost").strip()
   287	    port = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432").strip()
   288	    db_name = os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "kavach_db").strip()
   289	    user = os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "kavach_user").strip()
   290	    password = quote_plus(_postgres_password())
   291	    return f"postgresql+psycopg://{quote_plus(user)}:{password}@{host}:{port}/{db_name}"
   292	
   293	
   294	# Backwards-compatible name; psycopg 3 uses the same URL for sync and async engines.
   295	get_postgres_async_url = get_postgres_url
   296	
   297	
   298	def get_async_engine() -> AsyncEngine:
   299	    """Get or initialize singleton AsyncEngine with bounded local pool."""
   300	    global _engine
   301	    if _engine is None:
   302	        url = get_postgres_async_url()
   303	        _engine = create_async_engine(
   304	            url,
   305	            pool_size=5,
   306	            max_overflow=10,
   307	            pool_timeout=30,
   308	            pool_recycle=1800,
   309	            pool_pre_ping=True,
   310	            echo=False,
   311	        )
   312	    return _engine
   313	
   314	
   315	def get_async_session_factory() -> async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]:
   316	    """Get or initialize async session factory."""
   317	    global _session_factory
   318	    if _session_factory is None:
   319	        engine = get_async_engine()
   320	        _session_factory = async_sessionmaker(
   321	            bind=engine,
   322	            class_=AsyncSession,
   323	            expire_on_commit=False,
   324	            autoflush=False,
   325	        )
   326	    return _session_factory
   327	
   328	
   329	@asynccontextmanager
   330	async def get_session() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, None]:
   331	    """
   332	    Context manager providing an isolated AsyncSession per task/transaction.
   333	    Never share sessions across concurrent tasks.
   334	    """
   335	    factory = get_async_session_factory()
   336	    async with factory() as session:
   337	        try:
   338	            yield session
   339	            await session.commit()
   340	        except Exception:
   341	            await session.rollback()
   342	            raise
   343	
   344	
   345	async def dispose_async_engine() -> None:
   346	    """Explicit shutdown lifecycle for async engine."""
   347	    global _engine, _session_factory
   348	    if _engine is not None:
   349	        await _engine.dispose()
   350	        _engine = None
   351	        _session_factory = None
   352	        logger.info("KAVACH 2.0 Async PostgreSQL engine disposed.")

--- [Step 18 | THINKING] ---
I'm checking that the ledger append commits properly before the caller sees the event, and confirming that even a cancellation during commit would still trigger a rollback via session close. Now I'm looking at adding a trigger for update/delete protection on the table.

The gap is TRUNCATE isn't covered, the artifacts table lacks an immutability trigger, and the foreign key restricts deletion of analysis runs. Since the app's DB user owns the tables, a compromised app could still drop triggers or disable them entirely, meaning the ledger has a single trust root rather than true separation. Now I'm moving on to review storage.

60	    60	    Carries complete chain-of-custody metadata: lineage, producer, broker execution ID,
61	    61	    and agent tool call ID.
62	    62	    """

64	    64	    run_id: str

82	    82	        v_clean = v.strip().lower()
83	    83	        if not _HEX_SHA256_RE.match(v_clean):
84	    84	            raise ValueError(f"Invalid SHA-256 digest format: {v!r}")

102	   102	"""
103	   103	KAVACH AI 2.0 - Storage Backends for Content-Addressed Storage (CAS).
104	   104	Implements bit-level immutability, directory sharding, atomic replacement,
105	   105	and sandbox staging.
106	   106	"""
107	   107	
108	   108	from __future__ import annotations
109	   109	import asyncio
110	   110	import hashlib
111	   111	import os
112	   112	import shutil
113	   113	import stat
114	   114	import tempfile
115	   115	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
116	   116	from pathlib import Path
117	   117	from typing import Dict, Optional
118	   118	
119	   119	from storage.models import ArtifactIntegrityError
120	   120	
121	   121	
122	   122	class ArtifactStorageBackend(ABC):
123	   123	    """Abstract interface for storing and retrieving content-addressed blobs."""
124	   124	
125	   125	    @abstractmethod
126	   126	    async def write(self, sha256: str, data: bytes) -> str:
127	   127	        """Store bytes under their SHA-256 digest. Returns relative store_key."""
128	   128	        ...
129	   129	
130	   130	    @abstractmethod
131	   131	    async def read(self, store_key: str, expected_sha256: Optional[str] = None) -> bytes:
132	   132	        """Retrieve stored bytes and verify integrity."""
133	   133	        ...
134	   134	
135	   135	    @abstractmethod
136	   136	    async def exists(self, store_key: str) -> bool:
137	   137	        """Check if store_key exists."""
138	   138	        ...
139	   139	
140	   140	    @abstractmethod
141	   141	    async def stage(self, store_key: str, dest_path: Path, read_only: bool = True) -> Path:
142	   142	        """Stage stored artifact to a destination path for sandbox execution."""
143	   143	        ...
144	   144	
145	   145	
146	   146	class FilesystemStorageBackend(ArtifactStorageBackend):
147	   147	    """
148	   148	    Local filesystem content-addressed storage.
149	   149	    Organizes blobs into sharded directories: cas/<sha256[:2]>/<sha256>.
150	   150	    Enforces immutable filesystem permissions (0o444) and atomic temp-file rename.
151	   151	    """
152	   152	
153	   153	    def __init__(self, root_dir: Path | str) -> None:
154	   154	        self.root_dir = Path(root_dir).resolve()
155	   155	        self.cas_dir = self.root_dir / "cas"
156	   156	        self.tmp_dir = self.root_dir / ".tmp"
157	   157	        self.cas_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
158	   158	        self.tmp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
159	   159	
160	   160	    def _get_path_for_key(self, store_key: str) -> Path:
161	   161	        resolved = (self.root_dir / store_key).resolve()
162	   162	        # Security invariant: prevent directory traversal outside root_dir. Path ancestry, not a
163	   163	        # string prefix: "/srv/cas_evil" starts with "/srv/cas" but is not inside it.
164	   164	        if resolved == self.root_dir or not resolved.is_relative_to(self.root_dir):
165	   165	            raise ValueError(f"Path traversal detected in store_key: {store_key}")
166	   166	        return resolved
167	   167	
168	   168	    def _make_store_key(self, sha256: str) -> str:
169	   169	        return f"cas/{sha256[:2]}/{sha256}"
170	   170	
171	   171	    async def write(self, sha256: str, data: bytes) -> str:
172	   172	        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync_write, sha256, data)
173	   173	
174	   174	    def _sync_write(self, sha256: str, data: bytes) -> str:
175	   175	        actual_hash = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
176	   176	        if actual_hash != sha256.lower():
177	   177	            raise ArtifactIntegrityError(
178	   178	                f"Data hash mismatch: expected {sha256}, calculated {actual_hash}"
179	   179	            )
180	   180	
181	   181	        store_key = self._make_store_key(sha256)
182	   182	        final_path = self._get_path_for_key(store_key)
183	   183	
184	   184	        if final_path.exists():
185	   185	            # Already exists: verify existing file integrity
186	   186	            with open(final_path, "rb") as f:
187	   187	                existing_bytes = f.read()
188	   188	            if hashlib.sha256(existing_bytes).hexdigest() == sha256:
189	   189	                return store_key
190	   190	            # Existing file corrupted: replace it
191	   191	            final_path.chmod(0o644)
192	   192	
193	   193	        final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
194	   194	
195	   195	        # Atomic write: write to temp file on same filesystem, flush & sync, then replace
196	   196	        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=self.tmp_dir, delete=False) as tf:
197	   197	            tf.write(data)
198	   198	            tf.flush()
199	   199	            os.fsync(tf.fileno())
200	   200	            temp_name = tf.name
201	   201	
202	   202	        try:
203	   203	            # Set read-only permissions on blob
204	   204	            os.chmod(temp_name, stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IRGRP | stat.S_IROTH)
205	   205	            os.replace(temp_name, final_path)
206	   206	        except Exception:
207	   207	            if os.path.exists(temp_name):
208	   208	                try:
209	   209	                    os.unlink(temp_name)
210	   210	                except OSError:
211	   211	                    pass
212	   212	            raise
213	   213	
214	   214	        return store_key
215	   215	
216	   216	    async def read(self, store_key: str, expected_sha256: Optional[str] = None) -> bytes:
217	   217	        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync_read, store_key, expected_sha256)
218	   218	
219	   219	    def _sync_read(self, store_key: str, expected_sha256: Optional[str] = None) -> bytes:
220	   220	        path = self._get_path_for_key(store_key)
221	   221	        if not path.is_file():
222	   222	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Artifact blob not found at store_key: {store_key}")
223	   223	
224	   224	        with open(path, "rb") as f:
225	   225	            data = f.read()
226	   226	
227	   227	        if expected_sha256:
228	   228	            actual_sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
229	   229	            if actual_sha != expected_sha256.lower():
230	   230	                raise ArtifactIntegrityError(
231	   231	                    f"Integrity check failed for {store_key}: expected {expected_sha256}, got {actual_sha}"
232	   232	                )
233	   233	        return data
234	   234	
235	   235	    async def exists(self, store_key: str) -> bool:
236	   236	        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync_exists, store_key)
237	   237	
238	   238	    def _sync_exists(self, store_key: str) -> bool:
239	   239	        try:
240	   240	            return self._get_path_for_key(store_key).is_file()
241	   241	        except ValueError:
242	   242	            return False
243	   243	
244	   244	    async def stage(self, store_key: str, dest_path: Path, read_only: bool = True) -> Path:
245	   245	        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._sync_stage, store_key, dest_path, read_only)
246	   246	
247	   247	    def _sync_stage(self, store_key: str, dest_path: Path, read_only: bool = True) -> Path:
248	   248	        src = self._get_path_for_key(store_key)
249	   249	        if not src.is_file():
250	   250	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source artifact not found for staging: {store_key}")
251	   251	
252	   252	        dest = dest_path.resolve()
253	   253	        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
254	   254	
255	   255	        if dest.exists():
256	   256	            dest.unlink()
257	   257	
258	   258	        # Always an independent copy, never a hardlink: a hardlink shares the CAS blob's inode, so a
259	   259	        # sandbox that chmods and rewrites its working copy would rewrite the canonical blob too.
260	   260	        shutil.copyfile(src, dest)
261	   261	
262	   262	        if read_only:
263	   263	            try:
264	   264	                dest.chmod(stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IRGRP | stat.S_IROTH)
265	   265	            except OSError:
266	   266	                pass
267	   267	
268	   268	        return dest
269	   269	
270	   270	
271	   271	class MemoryStorageBackend(ArtifactStorageBackend):
272	   272	    """
273	   273	    In-memory storage backend for high-speed, zero-IO unit tests.
274	   274	    """
275	   275	
276	   276	    def __init__(self) -> None:
277	   277	        self._blobs: Dict[str, bytes] = {}
278	   278	
279	   279	    def _make_store_key(self, sha256: str) -> str:
280	   280	        return f"mem/{sha256[:2]}/{sha256}"
281	   281	
282	   282	    async def write(self, sha256: str, data: bytes) -> str:
283	   283	        actual_hash = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
284	   284	        if actual_hash != sha256.lower():
285	   285	            raise ArtifactIntegrityError(
286	   286	                f"Data hash mismatch: expected {sha256}, calculated {actual_hash}"
287	   287	            )
288	   288	        store_key = self._make_store_key(sha256)
289	   289	        self._blobs[store_key] = data
290	   290	        return store_key
291	   291	
292	   292	    async def read(self, store_key: str, expected_sha256: Optional[str] = None) -> bytes:
293	   293	        if store_key not in self._blobs:
294	   294	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Artifact blob not found in memory: {store_key}")
295	   295	        data = self._blobs[store_key]
296	   296	        if expected_sha256:
297	   297	            actual_sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
298	   298	            if actual_sha != expected_sha256.lower():
299	   299	                raise ArtifactIntegrityError(
300	   300	                    f"Integrity check failed: expected {expected_sha256}, got {actual_sha}"
301	   301	                )
302	   302	        return data
303	   303	
304	   304	    async def exists(self, store_key: str) -> bool:
305	   305	        return store_key in self._blobs
306	   306	
307	   307	    async def stage(self, store_key: str, dest_path: Path, read_only: bool = True) -> Path:
308	   308	        data = await self.read(store_key)
309	   309	        dest_path = dest_path.resolve()
310	   310	        dest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
311	   311	        dest_path.write_bytes(data)
312	   312	        if read_only:
313	   313	            try:
314	   314	                dest_path.chmod(stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IRGRP | stat.S_IROTH)
315	   315	            except OSError:
316	   316	                pass
317	   317	        return dest_path
318	   318	"""
319	   319	KAVACH AI 2.0 - Canonical Content-Addressed ArtifactStore.
320	   320	Single source of truth for all investigation artifacts. Manages immutable CAS blobs,
321	   321	enforces run-scoped isolation, tracks complete chain-of-custody lineage, and resolves
322	   322	opaque handles (artifact://<id>).
323	   323	"""
324	   324	
325	   325	from __future__ import annotations
326	   326	import hashlib
327	   327	import io
328	   328	import logging
329	   329	import re
330	   330	import shutil
331	   331	import stat
332	   332	import uuid
333	   333	import zipfile
334	   334	from pathlib import Path
335	   335	from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple
336	   336	
337	   337	from storage.models import (
338	   338	    StoredArtifact,
339	   339	    ArtifactIntegrityError,
340	   340	    ArtifactNotFoundError,
341	   341	    ArtifactAccessViolation,
342	   342	)
343	   343	from storage.backend import ArtifactStorageBackend, FilesystemStorageBackend, MemoryStorageBackend
344	   344	
345	   345	logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.storage.store")
346	   346	
347	   347	_OPAQUE_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$")
348	   348	
349	   349	
350	   350	class ArtifactStore:
351	   351	    """
352	   352	    Content-addressed artifact repository.
353	   353	    Guarantees:
354	   354	    1. Bit-for-bit immutability via cryptographic CAS hashes.
355	   355	    2. Strict run isolation: an artifact ID is scoped to its run; run A cannot resolve run B's artifacts.
356	   356	    3. Chain of custody: parents, broker execution IDs, tool call IDs recorded.
357	   357	    4. Opaque handles: agents only ever interact with artifact://<id>.
358	   358	
359	   359	    Persistence: with a persistence service, PostgreSQL holds the canonical descriptors and a
360	   360	    persistence failure fails the operation. Without one, the store runs in explicit ephemeral mode
361	   361	    (descriptors live only in this process), which tests and offline tools use.
362	   362	    """
363	   363	
364	   364	    def __init__(
365	   365	        self,
366	   366	        backend: Optional[ArtifactStorageBackend] = None,
367	   367	        persistence_service: Optional[Any] = None,
368	   368	    ) -> None:
369	   369	        self.backend: ArtifactStorageBackend = backend or MemoryStorageBackend()
370	   370	        self.persistence_service = persistence_service
371	   371	        # (run_id, artifact_id) -> descriptor. Cache only when persistent; canonical when ephemeral.
372	   372	        self._artifacts: Dict[Tuple[str, str], StoredArtifact] = {}
373	   373	
374	   374	    @property
375	   375	    def persistent(self) -> bool:
376	   376	        return self.persistence_service is not None
377	   377	
378	   378	    @classmethod
379	   379	    def create_filesystem_store(
380	   380	        cls,
381	   381	        root_dir: Path | str,
382	   382	        persistence_service: Optional[Any] = None,
383	   383	    ) -> "ArtifactStore":
384	   384	        backend = FilesystemStorageBackend(root_dir)
385	   385	        return cls(backend=backend, persistence_service=persistence_service)
386	   386	
387	   387	    @classmethod
388	   388	    def create_memory_store(
389	   389	        cls,
390	   390	        persistence_service: Optional[Any] = None,
391	   391	    ) -> "ArtifactStore":
392	   392	        backend = MemoryStorageBackend()
393	   393	        return cls(backend=backend, persistence_service=persistence_service)
394	   394	
395	   395	    @staticmethod
396	   396	    def normalize_id(handle_or_id: str) -> str:
397	   397	        """Strip artifact:// and validate identifier syntax to prevent path traversal."""
398	   398	        cleaned = handle_or_id.strip()
399	   399	        if cleaned.startswith("artifact://"):
400	   400	            cleaned = cleaned[len("artifact://"):]
401	   401	
402	   402	        if not cleaned or len(cleaned) > 64 or not _OPAQUE_ID_RE.match(cleaned):
403	   403	            raise ArtifactNotFoundError(f"Invalid artifact handle format: '{handle_or_id}'")
404	   404	        return cleaned
405	   405	
406	   406	    _normalize_id = normalize_id
407	   407	
408	   408	    async def put_bytes(
409	   409	        self,
410	   410	        run_id: str,
411	   411	        content: bytes,
412	   412	        artifact_type: str,
413	   413	        producer: str,
414	   414	        producer_version: str = "1.0.0",
415	   415	        parent_artifact_ids: Optional[List[str]] = None,
416	   416	        produced_by_execution_id: Optional[str] = None,
417	   417	        produced_by_tool_call_id: Optional[str] = None,
418	   418	        mime_type: Optional[str] = "application/octet-stream",
419	   419	        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
420	   420	        artifact_id: Optional[str] = None,
421	   421	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
422	   422	        """
423	   423	        Store bytes into the content-addressed backend and register the artifact descriptor.
424	   424	
425	   425	        Re-registering an existing (run_id, artifact_id) with identical content is idempotent;
426	   426	        with different content it raises ArtifactIntegrityError (artifacts are immutable).
427	   427	        """
428	   428	        if not run_id:
429	   429	            raise ValueError("run_id is required to register an artifact")
430	   430	
431	   431	        art_id = self.normalize_id(artifact_id or f"art_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}")
432	   432	        parents = [self.normalize_id(p) for p in (parent_artifact_ids or [])]
433	   433	        sha256 = hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
434	   434	
435	   435	        existing = await self._lookup(run_id, art_id)
436	   436	        if existing is not None:
437	   437	            if existing.sha256 != sha256:
438	   438	                raise ArtifactIntegrityError(
439	   439	                    f"Artifact '{existing.handle}' in run '{run_id}' already holds different content"
440	   440	                )
441	   441	            return existing
442	   442	
443	   443	        store_key = await self.backend.write(sha256, content)
444	   444	        artifact = StoredArtifact(
445	   445	            artifact_id=art_id,
446	   446	            run_id=run_id,
447	   447	            sha256=sha256,
448	   448	            size_bytes=len(content),
449	   449	            mime_type=mime_type or "application/octet-stream",
450	   450	            artifact_type=artifact_type,
451	   451	            storage_uri=f"cas://{sha256}",
452	   452	            store_key=store_key,
453	   453	            parent_artifact_ids=parents,
454	   454	            produced_by_execution_id=produced_by_execution_id,
455	   455	            produced_by_tool_call_id=produced_by_tool_call_id,
456	   456	            producer=producer,
457	   457	            producer_version=producer_version,
458	   458	            metadata=dict(metadata or {}),
459	   459	        )
460	   460	
461	   461	        # Persist first: a descriptor that is not durably recorded is not a stored artifact.
462	   462	        if self.persistence_service is not None:
463	   463	            await self.persistence_service.save_artifact(artifact)
464	   464	        self._artifacts[(run_id, art_id)] = artifact
465	   465	
466	   466	        logger.debug(f"Registered artifact {artifact.handle} (SHA-256: {sha256[:12]}..., run: {run_id})")
467	   467	        return artifact
468	   468	
469	   469	    async def put_file(
470	   470	        self,
471	   471	        run_id: str,
472	   472	        source_path: Path | str,
473	   473	        artifact_type: str,
474	   474	        producer: str,
475	   475	        producer_version: str = "1.0.0",
476	   476	        parent_artifact_ids: Optional[List[str]] = None,
477	   477	        produced_by_execution_id: Optional[str] = None,
478	   478	        produced_by_tool_call_id: Optional[str] = None,
479	   479	        mime_type: Optional[str] = None,
480	   480	        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
481	   481	        artifact_id: Optional[str] = None,
482	   482	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
483	   483	        """
484	   484	        Ingest a file (or a directory, packed as a deterministic ZIP) into content-addressed storage.
485	   485	        """
486	   486	        path = Path(source_path).resolve()
487	   487	        meta = dict(metadata or {})
488	   488	        if path.is_dir():
489	   489	            content = pack_directory(path)
490	   490	            mime_type = "application/zip"
491	   491	            meta.update({"layout": "directory", "file_count": sum(1 for f in path.rglob("*") if f.is_file())})
492	   492	        elif path.is_file():
493	   493	            content = path.read_bytes()
494	   494	            meta.setdefault("file_suffix", path.suffix.lower()[:16])
495	   495	        else:
496	   496	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source file does not exist: {path}")
497	   497	
498	   498	        return await self.put_bytes(
499	   499	            run_id=run_id,
500	   500	            content=content,
501	   501	            artifact_type=artifact_type,
502	   502	            producer=producer,
503	   503	            producer_version=producer_version,
504	   504	            parent_artifact_ids=parent_artifact_ids,
505	   505	            produced_by_execution_id=produced_by_execution_id,
506	   506	            produced_by_tool_call_id=produced_by_tool_call_id,
507	   507	            mime_type=mime_type,
508	   508	            metadata=meta,
509	   509	            artifact_id=artifact_id,
510	   510	        )
511	   511	
512	   512	    async def _lookup(self, run_id: str, artifact_id: str) -> Optional[StoredArtifact]:
513	   513	        cached = self._artifacts.get((run_id, artifact_id))
514	   514	        if cached is not None or self.persistence_service is None:
515	   515	            return cached
516	   516	        # Database errors propagate: an unreachable store is not "artifact absent".
517	   517	        model = await self.persistence_service.get_artifact_model(run_id, artifact_id)
518	   518	        if model is None:
519	   519	            return None
520	   520	        artifact = stored_artifact_from_model(model)
521	   521	        self._artifacts[(run_id, artifact_id)] = artifact
522	   522	        return artifact
523	   523	
524	   524	    async def get_artifact(self, artifact_id: str, run_id: str) -> StoredArtifact:
525	   525	        """
526	   526	        Retrieve an artifact descriptor by ID or handle within one run.
527	   527	
528	   528	        Artifact IDs are run-scoped, so the run is mandatory. An ID that exists only in another
529	   529	        run raises ArtifactAccessViolation; an unknown ID raises ArtifactNotFoundError.
530	   530	        """
531	   531	        if not run_id:
532	   532	            raise ArtifactAccessViolation("run_id is required to resolve an artifact handle")
533	   533	        normalized = self.normalize_id(artifact_id)
534	   534	        artifact = await self._lookup(run_id, normalized)
535	   535	        if artifact is not None:
536	   536	            return artifact
537	   537	        if any(aid == normalized for (_, aid) in self._artifacts):
538	   538	            raise ArtifactAccessViolation(
539	   539	                f"Access denied: artifact 'artifact://{normalized}' does not belong to run '{run_id}'"
540	   540	            )
541	   541	        raise ArtifactNotFoundError(f"Artifact '{artifact_id}' not found in run '{run_id}'")
542	   542	
543	   543	    async def get_content(
544	   544	        self,
545	   545	        artifact_id: str,
546	   546	        run_id: str,
547	   547	        verify_hash: bool = True,
548	   548	    ) -> bytes:
549	   549	        """
550	   550	        Read the raw bytes of an artifact, validating its cryptographic digest.
551	   551	        """
552	   552	        art = await self.get_artifact(artifact_id, run_id=run_id)
553	   553	        expected = art.sha256 if verify_hash else None
554	   554	        return await self.backend.read(art.store_key, expected_sha256=expected)
555	   555	
556	   556	    async def stage_artifact(
557	   557	        self,
558	   558	        artifact_id: str,
559	   559	        dest_dir: Path | str,
560	   560	        run_id: str,
561	   561	        filename: Optional[str] = None,
562	   562	        read_only: bool = True,
563	   563	    ) -> Path:
564	   564	        """
565	   565	        Copy an artifact into a sandbox directory for execution (never a link to the CAS blob).
566	   566	        """
567	   567	        art = await self.get_artifact(artifact_id, run_id=run_id)
568	   568	        target_name = filename or f"{art.artifact_id}_{art.sha256[:8]}.bin"
569	   569	        dest_path = Path(dest_dir).resolve() / target_name
570	   570	        return await self.backend.stage(art.store_key, dest_path, read_only=read_only)
571	   571	
572	   572	    async def materialize(self, artifact_id: str, run_id: str, dest_dir: Path | str) -> Path:
573	   573	        """
574	   574	        Write a hash-verified working copy of an artifact under dest_dir and return its path.
575	   575	
576	   576	        File artifacts become <dest_dir>/<id><suffix>; directory artifacts are unpacked into
577	   577	        <dest_dir>/<id>/. The copy is independent of the CAS blob: modifying it cannot change
578	   578	        the canonical content.
579	   579	        """
580	   580	        art = await self.get_artifact(artifact_id, run_id=run_id)
581	   581	        data = await self.backend.read(art.store_key, expected_sha256=art.sha256)
582	   582	        root = Path(dest_dir).resolve()
583	   583	        root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
584	   584	        if art.metadata.get("layout") == "directory":
585	   585	            target = root / art.artifact_id
586	   586	            if target.exists():
587	   587	                shutil.rmtree(target)
588	   588	            unpack_directory(data, target)
589	   589	            return target
590	   590	        suffix = str(art.metadata.get("file_suffix") or "")
591	   591	        if not re.fullmatch(r"(\.[A-Za-z0-9]{1,15})?", suffix):
592	   592	            suffix = ""
593	   593	        target = root / f"{art.artifact_id}{suffix}"
594	   594	        if target.exists():
595	   595	            target.chmod(0o644)
596	   596	            target.unlink()
597	   597	        target.write_bytes(data)
598	   598	        target.chmod(stat.S_IRUSR | stat.S_IRGRP | stat.S_IROTH)
599	   599	        return target
600	   600	
601	   601	    async def resolve_handle(self, handle: str, run_id: str) -> StoredArtifact:
602	   602	        """
603	   603	        Resolve an agent-supplied artifact handle (artifact://<id>) within one run.
604	   604	        """
605	   605	        return await self.get_artifact(handle, run_id=run_id)
606	   606	
607	   607	    async def list_artifacts_for_run(self, run_id: str) -> List[StoredArtifact]:
608	   608	        """
609	   609	        List all artifacts of an investigation run, in creation order.
610	   610	
611	   611	        Persistent stores read PostgreSQL, so the listing survives a process restart.
612	   612	        """
613	   613	        if self.persistence_service is not None:
614	   614	            models = await self.persistence_service.list_artifact_models(run_id)
615	   615	            artifacts = [stored_artifact_from_model(m) for m in models]
616	   616	            for art in artifacts:
617	   617	                self._artifacts[(run_id, art.artifact_id)] = art
618	   618	        else:
619	   619	            artifacts = [a for (rid, _), a in self._artifacts.items() if rid == run_id]
620	   620	        return sorted(artifacts, key=lambda a: a.created_at)
621	   621	
622	   622	    async def verify_integrity(self, artifact_id: str, run_id: str) -> bool:
623	   623	        """
624	   624	        Check that the stored blob for an artifact has not suffered bit rot or tampering.
625	   625	        """
626	   626	        try:
627	   627	            await self.get_content(artifact_id, run_id=run_id, verify_hash=True)
628	   628	            return True
629	   629	        except (ArtifactIntegrityError, FileNotFoundError):
630	   630	            return False
631	   631	
632	   632	
633	   633	# Fixed timestamp so identical directory trees always pack to identical bytes (same SHA-256).
634	   634	_ZIP_EPOCH = (1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
635	   635	MAX_UNPACKED_BYTES = 512 * 1024 * 1024
636	   636	
637	   637	
638	   638	def pack_directory(directory: Path) -> bytes:
639	   639	    """Pack a directory tree into a deterministic ZIP (sorted entries, fixed timestamps)."""
640	   640	    buf = io.BytesIO()
641	   641	    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
642	   642	        for path in sorted(p for p in directory.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()):
643	   643	            info = zipfile.ZipInfo(path.relative_to(directory).as_posix(), date_time=_ZIP_EPOCH)
644	   644	            info.external_attr = 0o444 << 16
645	   645	            info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
646	   646	            zf.writestr(info, path.read_bytes())
647	   647	    return buf.getvalue()
648	   648	
649	   649	
650	   650	def unpack_directory(data: bytes, target: Path) -> None:
651	   651	    """Unpack a directory artifact, rejecting entries that would escape target (zip slip)."""
652	   652	    target = target.resolve()
653	   653	    target.mkdir(parents=True)
654	   654	    total = 0
655	   655	    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as zf:
656	   656	        for info in zf.infolist():
657	   657	            dest = (target / info.filename).resolve()
658	   658	            if dest == target or not dest.is_relative_to(target):
659	   659	                raise ArtifactIntegrityError(f"Directory artifact entry escapes its root: {info.filename!r}")
660	   660	            total += info.file_size
661	   661	            if total > MAX_UNPACKED_BYTES:
662	   662	                raise ArtifactIntegrityError("Directory artifact exceeds the unpack size limit")
663	   663	            if info.is_dir():
664	   664	                dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
665	   665	                continue
666	   666	            dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
667	   667	            dest.write_bytes(zf.read(info))
668	   668	
669	   669	
670	   670	def stored_artifact_from_model(model: Any) -> StoredArtifact:
671	   671	    """Rebuild a StoredArtifact from its persisted ArtifactModel row."""
672	   672	    meta = dict(model.metadata_json or {})
673	   673	    return StoredArtifact(
674	   674	        artifact_id=model.handle_id,
675	   675	        run_id=model.run_id,
676	   676	        sha256=model.sha256,
677	   677	        size_bytes=model.size_bytes,
678	   678	        mime_type=model.mime_type,
679	   679	        artifact_type=model.artifact_type,
680	   680	        storage_uri=model.storage_uri,
681	   681	        store_key=meta.pop("store_key"),
682	   682	        parent_artifact_ids=meta.pop("parent_artifact_ids", []),
683	   683	        produced_by_execution_id=meta.pop("produced_by_execution_id", None),
684	   684	        produced_by_tool_call_id=meta.pop("produced_by_tool_call_id", None),
685	   685	        producer=model.producer,
686	   686	        producer_version=model.producer_version,
687	   687	        created_at=model.created_at,
688	   688	        metadata=meta,
689	   689	    )
690	

--- [Step 22 | THINKING] ---
I'm examining ArtifactStore issues: parent lineage isn't actually validated for existence or run membership, allowing forged or cross-run parents, and there's a check-then-act race on put_bytes where concurrent writes with the same run/artifact ID but different content would hit a database unique constraint, but that failure surfaces as a raw IntegrityError rather than a proper application-level error.

I'm also noting that store_key path traversal is bounded but reads verify sha256 against the descriptor so that's safe — however the staging path (_sync_stage) copies blobs into the sandbox without any hash verification, and there's an unverified get_content option too, which is a real gap. On immutability, blobs are chmod 0444 but owned by the same user, so any process running as that user could chmod and overwrite them — a hostile parser could tamper with blobs even though reads re-verify hashes against the DB afterward.

Beyond that, the artifacts table has no trigger preventing the sha256 column from being updated, so an attacker could swap the blob and update the DB descriptor together, leaving no detectable mismatch — and since the integrity report pulls from `list_artifacts_for_run` in the DB rather than cross-checking against the ledger's ARTIFACT_INGESTED sha256, that discrepancy would go unnoticed. I also check the access-violation logic in `get_artifact`, which only consults the in-memory cache, and look at how zip unpacking bounds decompressed size — CPython's ZipExtFile truncates reads to the declared file_size, so that risk is contained, and symlinks aren't created during extraction either.

Now I'm worried about pack_directory: it reads directory contents fully into memory with no size bound, which is risky for large JADX output on big APKs, and I'm examining whether `Path.rglob` follows symlinks to subdirectories across Python versions — the symlink-following behavior for `**` glob patterns changed in 3.13 with the `recurse_symlinks` parameter, so earlier versions' default behavior needs checking for potential infinite recursion or escape issues.

I'm also considering hardlinks inside a sandboxed output dir pointing to host files on the same filesystem (like CAS blobs), which could be an edge case since `is_symlink()` checks wouldn't catch a hardlink. The materialize target path construction looks fine, so now I'm moving on to review the persistence models and service layer.

    45	    @classmethod
    46	    async def create_sample(cls, sample: Sample) -> SampleModel:
    47	        """Create or get existing sample record by sha256."""
    48	        async with get_session() as session:
    49	            stmt = select(SampleModel).where(SampleModel.sha256 == sample.sha256)
    50	            res = await session.execute(stmt)
    51	            existing = res.scalar_one_or_none()
    52	            if existing:
    53	                return existing
    54	
    55	            ingested_dt = sample.ingested_at if isinstance(sample.ingested_at, datetime) else datetime.fromisoformat(str(sample.ingested_at))
    56	
    57	            model = SampleModel(
    58	                id=sample.id,
    59	                sha256=sample.sha256,
    60	                md5=sample.md5,
    61	                sha1=sample.sha1,
    62	                file_size_bytes=sample.file_size_bytes,
    63	                file_name=sample.file_name,
    64	                package_name=sample.package_name,
    65	                version_name=sample.version_name,
    66	                version_code=sample.version_code,
    67	                min_sdk=sample.min_sdk,
    68	                target_sdk=sample.target_sdk,
    69	                storage_path=sample.storage_path,
    70	                ingested_at=ingested_dt,
    71	                uploader_id=sample.uploader_id,
    72	                metadata_json=sample.metadata,
    73	            )
    74	            session.add(model)
    75	            await session.flush()
    76	            return model
    77	
    78	    @classmethod
    79	    async def create_analysis_run(cls, run: AnalysisRun) -> AnalysisRunModel:
    80	        """Register a new analysis run."""
    81	        async with get_session() as session:
    82	            started_dt = run.started_at if isinstance(run.started_at, datetime) else (datetime.fromisoformat(str(run.started_at)) if run.started_at else None)
    83	
    84	            model = AnalysisRunModel(
    85	                id=run.id,
    86	                sample_id=run.sample_id,
    87	                status=run.status.value if hasattr(run.status, "value") else str(run.status),
    88	                profile=run.profile.value if hasattr(run.profile, "value") else str(run.profile),
    89	                started_at=started_dt,
    90	                completed_at=None,
    91	                duration_seconds=None,
    92	                orchestrator_version=run.orchestrator_version,
    93	                kavach_git_commit=run.kavach_git_commit,
    94	                profile_version=run.profile_version,
    95	                configuration_hash=run.configuration_hash,
    96	                risk_policy_version=run.risk_policy_version,
    97	                config_overrides=run.config_overrides,
    98	                error_message=run.error_message,
    99	                metadata_json=run.metadata,
   100	            )
   101	            session.add(model)
   102	            await session.flush()
   103	            return model
   104	
   105	    @classmethod
   106	    async def record_stage_execution(cls, stage: StageExecution) -> StageExecutionModel:
   107	        """Record granular stage completion, resources, and execution state per attempt."""
   108	        async with get_session() as session:
   109	            started_dt = stage.started_at if isinstance(stage.started_at, datetime) else (datetime.fromisoformat(str(stage.started_at)) if stage.started_at else None)
   110	            completed_dt = stage.completed_at if isinstance(stage.completed_at, datetime) else (datetime.fromisoformat(str(stage.completed_at)) if stage.completed_at else None)
   111	            status_val = stage.status.value if hasattr(stage.status, "value") else str(stage.status)
   112	
   113	            stmt = select(StageExecutionModel).where(
   114	                StageExecutionModel.run_id == stage.run_id,
   115	                StageExecutionModel.stage_name == stage.stage_name,
   116	                StageExecutionModel.attempt == stage.attempt,
   117	            )
   118	            res = await session.execute(stmt)
   119	            existing = res.scalar_one_or_none()
   120	
   121	            if existing:
   122	                existing.status = status_val
   123	                existing.completed_at = completed_dt
   124	                existing.duration_seconds = stage.duration_seconds
   125	                existing.exit_code = stage.exit_code
   126	                existing.error_message = stage.error_message
   127	                existing.evidence_count = stage.evidence_count
   128	                existing.resource_usage = stage.resource_usage
   129	                existing.coverage_metadata = stage.coverage_metadata
   130	                return existing
   131	
   132	            model = StageExecutionModel(
   133	                id=stage.id,
   134	                run_id=stage.run_id,
   135	                stage_name=stage.stage_name,
   136	                attempt=stage.attempt,
   137	                tool_name=stage.tool_name,
   138	                tool_version=stage.tool_version,
   139	                status=status_val,
   140	                started_at=started_dt,
   141	                completed_at=completed_dt,
   142	                duration_seconds=stage.duration_seconds,
   143	                exit_code=stage.exit_code,
   144	                error_message=stage.error_message,
   145	                evidence_count=stage.evidence_count,
   146	                resource_usage=stage.resource_usage,
   147	                coverage_metadata=stage.coverage_metadata,
   148	            )
   149	            session.add(model)
   150	            await session.flush()
   151	            return model

   153	    @classmethod
   154	    async def save_evidence_batch(
   155	        cls, run_id: str, stage_execution_id: Optional[str], items: List[Evidence]
   156	    ) -> None:
   157	        """Atomically persist a batch of evidence items produced by an analyzer."""
   158	        if not items:
   159	            return
   160	
   161	        async with get_session() as session:
   162	            for item in items:
   163	                loc_dict = item.location.model_dump() if item.location else None
   164	                ts_dt = item.timestamp if isinstance(item.timestamp, datetime) else datetime.fromisoformat(str(item.timestamp))
   165	
   166	                model = EvidenceItemModel(
   167	                    id=item.id,
   168	                    run_id=run_id,
   169	                    stage_execution_id=stage_execution_id,
   170	                    origin=item.origin.value if hasattr(item.origin, "value") else str(item.origin),
   171	                    stage=item.stage,
   172	                    analyzer=item.analyzer,
   173	                    analyzer_version=item.analyzer_version,
   174	                    category=item.category,
   175	                    type=item.type,
   176	                    title=item.title,
   177	                    description=item.description,
   178	                    severity=item.severity.value if hasattr(item.severity, "value") else str(item.severity),
   179	                    confidence=item.confidence.value if hasattr(item.confidence, "value") else str(item.confidence),
   180	                    source=item.source,
   181	                    artifact=item.artifact,
   182	                    location=loc_dict,
   183	                    timestamp=ts_dt,
   184	                    normalized_value=str(item.normalized_value) if item.normalized_value is not None else None,
   185	                    raw_reference=item.raw_reference if isinstance(item.raw_reference, dict) else {},
   186	                    metadata_json=item.metadata,
   187	                )
   188	                session.add(model)
   189	            await session.flush()
   190	
   191	    @classmethod
   192	    async def save_risk_assessment(cls, risk: RiskAssessment) -> RiskAssessmentModel:
   193	        """Persist authoritative deterministic risk evaluation."""
   194	        async with get_session() as session:
   195	            threat_val = risk.threat_level.value if hasattr(risk.threat_level, "value") else str(risk.threat_level)
   196	            contributors = [c.model_dump() for c in risk.top_contributors]
   197	            eval_dt = risk.evaluated_at if isinstance(risk.evaluated_at, datetime) else datetime.fromisoformat(str(risk.evaluated_at))
   198	
   199	            model = RiskAssessmentModel(
   200	                id=risk.id,
   201	                run_id=risk.run_id,
   202	                composite_score=risk.composite_score,
   203	                threat_level=threat_val,
   204	                policy_name=risk.policy_name,
   205	                policy_version=risk.policy_version,
   206	                sub_scores=risk.sub_scores,
   207	                score_weights=risk.score_weights,
   208	                top_contributors=contributors,
   209	                summary_verdict=risk.summary_verdict,
   210	                evaluated_at=eval_dt,
   211	                metadata_json=risk.metadata,
   212	            )
   213	            session.add(model)
   214	            await session.flush()
   215	            return model
   216	
   217	    @classmethod
   218	    async def save_forensic_report(cls, report: ForensicReport) -> ReportModel:
   219	        """Save canonical structured report."""
   220	        async with get_session() as session:
   221	            created_dt = report.generated_at if isinstance(report.generated_at, datetime) else datetime.fromisoformat(str(report.generated_at))
   222	            model = ReportModel(
   223	                id=report.id,
   224	                run_id=report.run_id,
   225	                schema_version="2.0.0",
   226	                generator_type=report.generator_type,
   227	                generator_version="2.0.0",
   228	                report_json=report.model_dump(mode="json"),
   229	                rendered_markdown=report.rendered_formats.get("MARKDOWN"),
   230	                created_at=created_dt,
   231	            )
   232	            session.add(model)
   233	            await session.flush()
   234	            return model
   235	
   236	    @classmethod
   237	    async def finalize_analysis_run(
   238	        cls, run_id: str, status: ExecutionState, error_message: Optional[str] = None
   239	    ) -> None:
   240	        """
   241	        Finalize run execution lifecycle and record timing.
   242	        """
   243	        async with get_session() as session:
   244	            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   245	            run_res = await session.execute(
   246	                select(AnalysisRunModel).where(AnalysisRunModel.id == run_id)
   247	            )
   248	            run = run_res.scalar_one_or_none()
   249	            if run:
   250	                run.status = status.value if hasattr(status, "value") else str(status)
   251	                run.completed_at = now
   252	                if run.started_at:
   253	                    duration = (now - run.started_at).total_seconds()
   254	                    run.duration_seconds = max(0.0, duration)
   255	                if error_message:
   256	                    run.error_message = error_message
   257	
   258	            logger.info(f"Finalized analysis run {run_id} with status {status}")
   259	
   260	    @classmethod
   261	    async def save_artifact(
   262	        cls, artifact: StoredArtifact, stage_execution_id: Optional[str] = None
   263	    ) -> ArtifactModel:
   264	        """Insert an ArtifactStore descriptor. Artifacts are immutable: there is no update path."""
   265	        created_dt = (
   266	            artifact.created_at
   267	            if isinstance(artifact.created_at, datetime)
   268	            else datetime.fromisoformat(str(artifact.created_at))
   269	        )
   270	        async with get_session() as session:
   271	            model = ArtifactModel(
   272	                id=f"art_{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
   273	                run_id=artifact.run_id,
   274	                handle_id=artifact.artifact_id,
   275	                stage_execution_id=stage_execution_id,
   276	                artifact_type=artifact.artifact_type,
   277	                sha256=artifact.sha256,
   278	                size_bytes=artifact.size_bytes,
   279	                mime_type=artifact.mime_type,
   280	                storage_uri=artifact.storage_uri,
   281	                producer=artifact.producer,
   282	                producer_version=artifact.producer_version,
   283	                created_at=created_dt,
   284	                metadata_json=artifact.to_metadata_dict(),
   285	            )
   286	            session.add(model)
   287	            await session.flush()
   288	            return model
   289	
   290	    @classmethod
   291	    async def get_artifact_model(cls, run_id: str, handle_id: str) -> Optional[ArtifactModel]:
   292	        """Fetch one run's artifact by its run-scoped handle ID."""
   293	        async with get_session() as session:
   294	            stmt = select(ArtifactModel).where(
   295	                ArtifactModel.run_id == run_id, ArtifactModel.handle_id == handle_id
   296	            )
   297	            res = await session.execute(stmt)
   298	            return res.scalar_one_or_none()
   299	
   300	    @classmethod
   301	    async def list_artifact_models(cls, run_id: str) -> List[ArtifactModel]:
   302	        """List the ArtifactStore artifacts of an analysis run in creation order."""
   303	        async with get_session() as session:
   304	            stmt = (
   305	                select(ArtifactModel)
   306	                .where(ArtifactModel.run_id == run_id, ArtifactModel.handle_id.is_not(None))
   307	                .order_by(ArtifactModel.created_at, ArtifactModel.id)
   308	            )
   309	            res = await session.execute(stmt)
   310	            return list(res.scalars().all())

--- [Step 24 | THINKING] ---
I'm noting that the sha256 unique constraint migration drops a redundant key but an index-based unique constraint likely still exists, so that should be fine. I'm also thinking through a race condition in create_sample where two concurrent uploads of the same sample could both pass the existence check and then collide on insert, causing an integrity error on the second one.

Now I'm checking artifact and ledger foreign key cascade behavior — analysis run deletion cascades to evidence but ledger events use RESTRICT, which seems correct. I want to look at the remaining models to confirm this.

class ArtifactModel(Base):
    """Metadata and filesystem/storage URI pointers for generated artifacts."""
    __tablename__ = "artifacts"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("idx_artifacts_run_type", "run_id", "artifact_type"),

    )

    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)
    run_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), ForeignKey("analysis_runs.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True
    )

        String(64), ForeignKey("stage_executions.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True, index=True

    handle_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)

    )

class LedgerEventModel(Base):
    """Append-only, hash-chained InvestigationLedger events (UPDATE/DELETE rejected by a trigger).

    Every hashed field is stored as text exactly as hashed (ISO timestamp, canonical payload JSON),
    so verification recomputes the hash from these columns without any database type round-trip.
    """
    __tablename__ = "ledger_events"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint("run_id", "sequence", name="uq_ledger_events_run_sequence"),
    )

    run_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), ForeignKey("analysis_runs.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"), nullable=False, index=True
    )

    causation_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    correlation_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    event_timestamp: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(40), nullable=False)
    payload_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    prev_event_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    event_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)

--- [Step 26 | THINKING] ---
I notice artifacts can cascade-delete from analysis_runs, but ledger entries use RESTRICT, so runs with ledger events can't be removed. More concerning: artifact rows themselves are mutable and directly deletable, which would shrink the report's artifact list without failing verification, since the ledger never cross-checks ARTIFACT_CREATED hashes against the actual artifact descriptors.

     2	Autonomous Forensic Investigation Controller for KAVACH AI 2.0.
     3	Executes the evidence-driven state machine:
     4	OBSERVE -> HYPOTHESIZE -> PLAN -> ACT -> INSPECT -> VERIFY -> UPDATE LEDGER -> REPLAN -> FINALIZE.
     5	
     6	Enforces:
     7	1. Supervisor Frontier Gate: Finalization is an admissible request, never an unconditional
     8	   model completion. Rejects finalization while an actionable investigation frontier remains.
     9	2. Multi-Tool Dispatcher (Serial Pre-Scheduler): Validates and executes all N tool calls
