    74	
    75	class ToolRegistry:
    76	    def __init__(self):
    77	        self._tools: Dict[str, BaseTool] = {}
    78	        self._type_adapter = TypeAdapter(ToolCallInput)
    79	
    80	    def register(self, tool: BaseTool) -> None:
    81	        self._tools[tool.name] = tool
    82	        logger.info(f"Registered tool: {tool.name} (v{tool.version}, category: {tool.category})")
    83	
    84	    def register_tool(self, tool: BaseTool) -> None:
    85	        self.register(tool)
    86	
    87	    def get_tool(self, name: str) -> Optional[BaseTool]:
    88	        return self._tools.get(name)
    89	
    90	    def list_tools(self) -> List[str]:
    91	        """Return list of all registered tool names."""
    92	        return list(self._tools.keys())
    93	
    94	    def _get_schema_for_tool(self, tool: BaseTool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    95	        input_model = TOOL_INPUT_MODELS.get(tool.name)
    96	        if input_model:
    97	            model_schema = input_model.model_json_schema()
    98	            properties = {k: v for k, v in model_schema.get("properties", {}).items() if k != "tool"}
    99	            required = [r for r in model_schema.get("required", []) if r != "tool"]
   100	            parameters = {
   101	                "type": "object",
   102	                "properties": properties,
   103	                "required": required,
   104	            }
   105	        else:
   106	            parameters = {
   107	                "type": "object",
   108	                "properties": {
   109	                    "artifact_id": {"type": "string", "description": "Target file or artifact path"},
   110	                },
   111	            }
   112	        return {
   113	            "type": "function",
   114	            "function": {
   115	                "name": tool.name,
   116	                "description": tool.description,
   117	                "parameters": parameters,
   118	            },
   119	        }
   120	
   121	    def get_tools_schema(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   122	        """Returns OpenAI-compatible function calling schemas for all registered tools."""
   123	        return [self._get_schema_for_tool(tool) for tool in self._tools.values()]
   124	
   125	    def discover_capabilities(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   126	        grouped: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
   127	        for tool in self._tools.values():
   128	            cat = tool.family.value.lower()
   129	            if cat not in grouped:
   130	                grouped[cat] = []
   131	            grouped[cat].append(tool.name)
   132	        if "reconstruction" in grouped and "repair_container" not in grouped["reconstruction"]:
   133	            grouped["reconstruction"].append("repair_container")
   134	        return grouped
   135	
   136	    def list_capabilities(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   137	        """Alias for discover_capabilities for contract compatibility."""
   138	        return self.discover_capabilities()
   139	
   140	    def discover_tools(self, family: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   141	        fam_norm = family.strip().lower()
   142	        tools = []
   143	        for tool in self._tools.values():
   144	            t_fam = tool.family.value.lower()
   145	            if t_fam == fam_norm or (fam_norm == "reconstruction" and tool.name == "repair_container"):
   146	                tools.append({
   147	                    "name": tool.name,
   148	                    "description": tool.description,
   149	                })
   150	        return tools
   151	
   152	    def get_tool_schema(self, tool_name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   153	        tool = self.get_tool(tool_name)
   154	        if not tool:
   155	            return None
   156	        return self._get_schema_for_tool(tool)
   157	
   158	    def validate_input(self, raw_input: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolCallInput:
   159	        """Validates raw dictionary against discriminated union ToolCallInput."""
   160	        return self._type_adapter.validate_python(raw_input)
   161	
   162	    async def execute_tool(self, raw_input: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
   163	        """Validate tool input, then execute the registered tool.
   164	
   165	        Fail-closed: input that does not validate against the tool's schema is rejected
   166	        and the tool's execute() is never entered.
   167	        """
   168	        tool_name = raw_input.get("tool") if isinstance(raw_input, dict) else None
   169	
   170	        try:
   171	            validated_input: ToolCallInput = self.validate_input(raw_input)
   172	        except ValidationError as ve:
   173	            logger.warning(f"Rejected tool call '{tool_name}': input validation failed ({ve.error_count()} errors)")
   174	            return ToolOutput(
   175	                tool_name=str(tool_name or "unknown"),
   176	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   177	                duration_seconds=0.0,
   178	                output_summary=f"Input validation failed for tool '{tool_name}'; the tool was not executed: {ve}",
   179	                error="INPUT_VALIDATION_FAILED",
   180	            )
   181	
   182	        tool = self.get_tool(validated_input.tool)
   183	        if not tool:
   184	            return ToolOutput(
   185	                tool_name=validated_input.tool,
   186	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   187	                duration_seconds=0.0,
   188	                output_summary=f"Tool '{validated_input.tool}' is not registered in ToolRegistry",
   189	                error="TOOL_NOT_FOUND",
   190	            )
   191	
   192	        if not await tool.is_available():
   193	            return ToolOutput(
   194	                tool_name=tool.name,
   195	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SKIPPED,
   196	                duration_seconds=0.0,
   197	                output_summary=f"Tool '{tool.name}' is currently unavailable in the environment",
   198	                error="TOOL_UNAVAILABLE",
   199	            )
   200	
   201	        return await tool.execute(validated_input, context)
   202	
   203	
   204	# Self-repair tools mutate the KAVACH source tree (patch, test, commit, rollback).
   205	# They are registered ONLY in the repair registry; the investigation registry must never
   206	# contain them. Registration is the authorization boundary, not prompt instructions.
   207	REPAIR_TOOL_NAMES = frozenset({
   208	    "apply_patch",
   209	    "run_scoped_pytest",
   210	    "git_diff",
   211	    "git_checkpoint",
   212	    "rollback",
   213	    "verify_repair_session",
   214	})
   215	
   216	
   217	def build_investigation_tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
   218	    """Registry for forensic investigations: inspection, discovery, reconstruction, sandboxed workbench.
   219	
   220	    Execution plane: every external process launched by these tools goes through the ExecutionBroker
   221	    (run_jadx and inspect_elf_symbols via tools/brokered.py; python_exec/shell_exec via workbench/tools.py).
   222	    The remaining tools (inspect_container, repair_container, search_strings, find_xrefs,
   223	    find_payload_candidates, decode_static_payload, meta and file tools) are in-process Python parsers
   224	    with no external executable, so there is no process to broker; they parse hostile bytes in the
   225	    KAVACH process, which the ArtifactStore/execution phases may move out of process. Quark, Androguard,
   226	    APKiD and YARA-X run only in the deterministic orchestrator and are not agent-reachable.
   227	    test_forensic_execution_broker.py statically forbids direct process APIs under tools/.
   228	    """
   229	    from tools.forensic.jadx_tool import JadxTool
   230	    from tools.forensic.elf_native_tool import ElfNativeTool
   231	    from tools.forensic.inspect_container_tool import InspectContainerTool
   232	    from tools.discovery.search_strings_tool import SearchStringsTool
   233	    from tools.discovery.find_xrefs_tool import FindXrefsTool
   234	    from tools.discovery.find_payload_candidates_tool import FindPayloadCandidatesTool
   235	    from tools.reconstruction.container_repair_tool import ContainerRepairTool
   236	    from tools.reconstruction.payload_decoder_tool import PayloadDecoderTool
   237	    from tools.meta.registry_tools import DiscoverCapabilitiesTool, DiscoverToolsTool, GetToolSchemaTool
   238	    from tools.meta.inspect_state_tool import InspectStateTool
   239	    from workbench.tools import (
   240	        PythonExecTool,
   241	        ShellExecTool,
   242	        ReadFileTool,
   243	        WriteFileTool,
   244	        CreateDerivedArtifactTool,
   245	        ListDirectoryTool,
   246	        InspectArtifactTool,
   247	    )
   248	
   249	    registry = ToolRegistry()
   250	    registry.register(InspectContainerTool())
   251	    registry.register(ContainerRepairTool())
   252	    registry.register(JadxTool())
   253	    registry.register(ElfNativeTool())
   254	    registry.register(SearchStringsTool())
   255	    registry.register(FindXrefsTool())
   256	    registry.register(FindPayloadCandidatesTool())
   257	    registry.register(PayloadDecoderTool())
   258	
   259	    registry.register(DiscoverCapabilitiesTool(registry))
   260	    registry.register(DiscoverToolsTool(registry))
   261	    registry.register(GetToolSchemaTool(registry))
   262	    registry.register(InspectStateTool())
   263	
   264	    # Workbench Tools
   265	    registry.register(PythonExecTool())
   266	    registry.register(ShellExecTool())
   267	    registry.register(ReadFileTool())
   268	    registry.register(WriteFileTool())
   269	    registry.register(CreateDerivedArtifactTool())
   270	    registry.register(ListDirectoryTool())
   271	    registry.register(InspectArtifactTool())
   272	
   273	    leaked = REPAIR_TOOL_NAMES & set(registry.list_tools())
   274	    if leaked:
   275	        raise RuntimeError(f"Repair tools must not be registered for investigations: {sorted(leaked)}")
   276	    return registry
   277	
   278	
   279	DEMO_TOOL_NAMES = frozenset({
   280	    "inspect_container",
   281	    "search_strings",
   282	    "run_jadx",
   283	    "find_xrefs",
   284	    "decode_static_payload",
   285	    "repair_container",
   286	})
   287	
   288	
   289	def build_demo_tool_registry() -> ToolRegistry:
   290	    """Registry for the InvestigationService demo: only the strongest stable forensic tools.
   291	
   292	    Registration is the authorization boundary: no workbench (python_exec/shell_exec), file or meta
   293	    tools are registered, so the model cannot reach them whatever it emits.
   294	    """
   295	    full = build_investigation_tool_registry()

   322	
   323	# Global investigation registry instance (never contains repair tools)
   324	global_tool_registry = build_investigation_tool_registry()
   325	"""
   326	External-process execution for agent-reachable forensic tools.
   327	
   328	Every external executable a forensic tool launches goes through the ExecutionBroker: policy
   329	resolution (isolation tier, network clamp, timeout ceiling), the workbench allowlist and argument
   330	validators, and the bubblewrap backend. Tools never call subprocess APIs directly.
   331	
   332	The resolved artifact is staged into the run's FORENSIC_WORKSPACE so the sandbox can read it, and
   333	is passed to the command as a path relative to the sandbox working directory; host paths never
   334	appear in the argv.
   335	"""
   336	
   337	from __future__ import annotations
   338	import shutil
   339	from pathlib import Path
   340	from typing import Any, Dict, List
   341	
   342	from execution.models import ExecutionRequest, ExecutionResult, IsolationTier
   343	from workbench.tools import _get_or_create_execution_broker, _get_or_create_resolver
   344	
   345	SANDBOX_CWD_URI = "forensic://"
   346	STAGING_DIR = "staged"
   347	
   348	
   349	def stage_artifact(context: Dict[str, Any], artifact_id: str, host_path: str) -> str:
   350	    """Copy a resolved artifact into FORENSIC_WORKSPACE/staged; return its sandbox-relative path.
   351	
   352	    Always a fresh copy, never a hardlink: the sandbox mounts FORENSIC_WORKSPACE read-write, so a
   353	    linked file would let a sandboxed process rewrite the store's working copy.
   354	    """
   355	    resolver = _get_or_create_resolver(context)
   356	    safe_id = artifact_id.removeprefix("artifact://").replace("/", "_")
   357	    dest = resolver.forensic_root / STAGING_DIR / f"{safe_id}{Path(host_path).suffix}"
   358	    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   359	    if dest.exists():
   360	        dest.unlink()
   361	    shutil.copyfile(host_path, dest)
   362	    return f"{STAGING_DIR}/{dest.name}"
   363	
   364	
   365	async def run_brokered(
   366	    context: Dict[str, Any],
   367	    tool_id: str,
   368	    tool_version: str,
   369	    argv: List[str],
   370	    artifact_id: str,
   371	    timeout_seconds: float,
   372	) -> ExecutionResult:
   373	    """Run an allowlisted external tool through the ExecutionBroker inside the forensic sandbox."""
   374	    resolver = _get_or_create_resolver(context)
   375	    broker = _get_or_create_execution_broker(context, resolver)
   376	    request = ExecutionRequest(
   377	        tool_id=tool_id,
   378	        tool_version=tool_version,
   379	        command=argv,
   380	        cwd_uri=SANDBOX_CWD_URI,
   381	        input_artifacts=[artifact_id.removeprefix("artifact://")],
   382	        network_policy="NETWORK_NONE",
   383	        requested_isolation=IsolationTier.HIGH,
   384	        timeout_seconds=timeout_seconds,
   385	    )
   386	    return await broker.execute(request)
   387	
   388	
   389	def sandbox_path(context: Dict[str, Any], relative: str) -> Path:
   390	    """Host location of a path the sandboxed command wrote relative to its working directory."""
   391	    return _get_or_create_resolver(context).forensic_root / relative
   392	
   393	
   394	def execution_provenance(result: ExecutionResult) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   395	    """Internal provenance record for ToolOutput.raw_data (never model-visible)."""
   396	    manifest = result.execution_manifest or {}
   397	    return {
   398	        "execution_id": result.execution_id,
   399	        "tool_id": manifest.get("tool_id"),
   400	        "tool_version": manifest.get("tool_version"),
   401	        "executable_sha256": manifest.get("environment_digest"),
   402	        "command_argv": manifest.get("command_argv"),
   403	        "input_hashes": manifest.get("input_hashes"),
   404	        "requested_isolation": result.requested_isolation.value,
   405	        "effective_isolation": result.effective_isolation.value,
   406	        "backend_id": result.backend_id,
   407	        "network_policy": manifest.get("network_policy"),
   408	        "status": result.status.value,
   409	        "exit_code": result.exit_code,
   410	        "timestamps": result.timestamps,
   411	        "duration_ms": result.metrics.get("duration_ms"),
   412	    }
   413	"""
   414	Run-bound view of the ArtifactStore used by the investigation controller and its tools.
   415	
   416	The ArtifactStore is the only source of artifact identity, content and lineage. This view binds it
   417	to one run and keeps a hash-verified working copy of each artifact on disk, because the forensic
   418	tools parse files. Working copies are independent copies of the CAS blobs: a tool or sandbox that
   419	modifies one cannot change canonical content, and a modified file copy is detected (SHA-256) on the
   420	next resolution, which then fails closed.
   421	"""
   422	
   423	from __future__ import annotations
   424	import hashlib
   425	from pathlib import Path
   426	from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional
   427	
   428	from storage.models import ArtifactIntegrityError, ArtifactNotFoundError, StoredArtifact
   429	from storage.store import ArtifactStore
   430	
   431	
   432	def _file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
   433	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
   434	    with open(path, "rb") as fh:
   435	        for chunk in iter(lambda: fh.read(1 << 20), b""):
   436	            digest.update(chunk)
   437	    return digest.hexdigest()
   438	
   439	
   440	class RunArtifacts:
   441	    """Artifacts of one investigation run: register into the store, resolve handles to working copies."""
   442	
   443	    def __init__(self, store: ArtifactStore, run_id: str, workspace: Path | str, path_resolver: Any = None) -> None:
   444	        if not run_id:
   445	            raise ValueError("RunArtifacts requires a run_id")
   446	        self.store = store
   447	        self.run_id = run_id
   448	        self.workspace = Path(workspace).resolve()
   449	        self.path_resolver = path_resolver
   450	        self._artifacts: Dict[str, StoredArtifact] = {}
   451	        self._paths: Dict[str, Path] = {}
   452	
   453	    async def load_existing(self) -> List[StoredArtifact]:
   454	        """Bind every artifact the store already holds for this run (for example after a restart)."""
   455	        artifacts = await self.store.list_artifacts_for_run(self.run_id)
   456	        for art in artifacts:
   457	            await self._bind(art)
   458	        return artifacts
   459	
   460	    async def register_file(
   461	        self,
   462	        source_path: Path | str,
   463	        artifact_type: str,
   464	        producer: str,
   465	        producer_version: str = "1.0.0",
   466	        artifact_id: Optional[str] = None,
   467	        parent_artifact_ids: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None,
   468	        produced_by_execution_id: Optional[str] = None,
   469	        produced_by_tool_call_id: Optional[str] = None,
   470	        mime_type: Optional[str] = None,
   471	        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   472	    ) -> StoredArtifact:
   473	        """Ingest a file or directory into the store for this run and bind its working copy."""
   474	        art = await self.store.put_file(
   475	            run_id=self.run_id,
   476	            source_path=source_path,
   477	            artifact_type=artifact_type,
   478	            producer=producer,
   479	            producer_version=producer_version,
   480	            parent_artifact_ids=list(parent_artifact_ids or []),
   481	            produced_by_execution_id=produced_by_execution_id,
   482	            produced_by_tool_call_id=produced_by_tool_call_id,
   483	            mime_type=mime_type,
   484	            metadata=metadata,
   485	            artifact_id=artifact_id,
   486	        )
   487	        await self._bind(art)
   488	        return art
   489	
   490	    async def _bind(self, art: StoredArtifact) -> None:
   491	        path = await self.store.materialize(art.artifact_id, self.run_id, self.workspace)
   492	        self._artifacts[art.artifact_id] = art
   493	        self._paths[art.artifact_id] = path
   494	        if self.path_resolver is not None:
   495	            self.path_resolver.register_artifact(art.artifact_id, path)
   496	
   497	    def ids(self) -> List[str]:
   498	        return list(self._artifacts)
   499	
   500	    def __contains__(self, artifact_id: object) -> bool:
   501	        return artifact_id in self._artifacts
   502	
   503	    def __iter__(self):
   504	        return iter(list(self._artifacts))
   505	
   506	    def __len__(self) -> int:
   507	        return len(self._artifacts)
   508	
   509	    def get(self, handle_or_id: str) -> StoredArtifact:
   510	        artifact_id = ArtifactStore.normalize_id(handle_or_id)
   511	        if artifact_id not in self._artifacts:
   512	            raise ArtifactNotFoundError(f"Unknown artifact handle 'artifact://{artifact_id}' for this investigation")
   513	        return self._artifacts[artifact_id]
   514	
   515	    def all(self) -> List[StoredArtifact]:
   516	        return list(self._artifacts.values())
   517	
   518	    def paths(self) -> Dict[str, Path]:
   519	        return dict(self._paths)
   520	
   521	    def resolve(self, handle_or_id: str) -> Path:
   522	        """Working-copy path for a handle of this run. The path never leaves the tool boundary.
   523	
   524	        Fails closed when a file working copy no longer matches its stored SHA-256.
   525	        """
   526	        art = self.get(handle_or_id)
   527	        path = self._paths[art.artifact_id]
   528	        if art.metadata.get("layout") != "directory" and (not path.is_file() or _file_sha256(path) != art.sha256):
   529	            raise ArtifactIntegrityError(f"Working copy of {art.handle} no longer matches its stored SHA-256")
   530	        return path

--- [Step 39 | THINKING] ---
The demo registry doesn't include get_tool_schema, which limits escalation here, but the full investigation registry allows get_tool_schema to unlock tools like python_exec through progressive discovery. That's intentional design rather than a privilege escalation issue, since all registered tools are already authorized.

I'm also looking at RunArtifacts.resolve for TOCTOU concerns — hash checks happen before reads but directory artifacts skip hash verification entirely. I want to check how register_file ingests tool-provided storage_uri paths, whether _get_or_create_execution_broker is a global singleton with concurrency implications, and review the demo tools themselves: inspect_container, search_strings, run_jadx, find_xrefs, decode_static_payload, and repair_container.

[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
     1	"""
     2	KAVACH AI 2.0 - APK Container Inspection & Evasion Audit Tool.
     3	Inspects ZIP/APK headers non-destructively for malformed compression methods,
     4	fake encryption flags, zero compressed sizes, and standard ZIP compliance.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	import os
     9	import zipfile
    10	import struct
    11	import time
    12	from pathlib import Path
    13	from typing import Dict, Any, List
    14	
    15	from tools.base import BaseTool, ToolFamily, InspectContainerInput, ToolOutput, ToolExecutionStatus
    16	from tools.base import ArtifactHandleError, artifact_handle_rejection, resolve_artifact_handle
    17	from domain.evidence import Evidence, EvidenceOrigin, EvidenceSeverity, EvidenceConfidence
    18	
    19	
    20	class InspectContainerTool(BaseTool):
    21	    name: str = "inspect_container"
    22	    family: ToolFamily = ToolFamily.CONTAINER
    23	    version: str = "1.0.0"
    24	    category: str = "container"
    25	    description: str = "Inspects APK/ZIP container headers, evasion techniques (non-standard compression, fake encryption flags), and entry integrity."
    26	
    27	    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
    28	        return True
    29	
    30	    async def execute(self, tool_input: InspectContainerInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
    31	        start_time = time.time()
    32	        try:
    33	            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
    34	        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
    35	            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)
    36	
    37	        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
    38	            return ToolOutput(
    39	                tool_name=self.name,
    40	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
    41	                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
    42	                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
    43	                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
    44	            )
    45	
    46	        p = Path(artifact_path)
    47	        raw_bytes = p.read_bytes()
    48	
    49	        anomalies: List[str] = []
    50	        entries: List[str] = []
    51	        dex_files: List[str] = []
    52	        has_manifest: bool = False
    53	
    54	        # Scan Local File Headers (PK\x03\x04)
    55	        offset = 0
    56	        while offset < len(raw_bytes) - 30:
    57	            if raw_bytes[offset:offset+4] == b"PK\x03\x04":
    58	                header_data = raw_bytes[offset:offset+30]
    59	                sig, ver, flag, comp, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, nlen, elen = struct.unpack("<4sHHHHHIIIHH", header_data)
    60	                fn_start = offset + 30
    61	                fn_end = fn_start + nlen
    62	                filename = raw_bytes[fn_start:fn_end].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    63	                entries.append(filename)
    64	
    65	                if filename == "AndroidManifest.xml":
    66	                    has_manifest = True
    67	                if filename.endswith(".dex"):
    68	                    dex_files.append(filename)
    69	
    70	                if flag & 0x0001:
    71	                    anomalies.append(f"{filename}: fake encryption flag (bit 0 set in local header)")
    72	
    73	                if comp not in (0, 8):
    74	                    anomalies.append(f"{filename}: non-standard compression method {comp} (local header)")
    75	
    76	                offset = fn_end + elen + csize if csize > 0 else fn_end + elen
    77	            else:
    78	                offset += 1
    79	
    80	        # Check standard zipfile readability
    81	        zip_valid = False
    82	        zip_error = None
    83	        try:
    84	            with zipfile.ZipFile(artifact_path, "r") as zf:
    85	                bad_file = zf.testzip()
    86	                if bad_file:
    87	                    anomalies.append(f"testzip() CRC failure on: {bad_file}")
    88	                else:
    89	                    zip_valid = True
    90	        except Exception as ze:
    91	            zip_error = str(ze)
    92	            anomalies.append(f"ZipFile parsing error: {ze}")
    93	
    94	        new_evidence: List[Evidence] = []
    95	        if anomalies:
    96	            new_evidence.append(
    97	                Evidence(
    98	                    run_id=context.get("run_id", "manual"),
    99	                    origin=EvidenceOrigin.STATIC,
   100	                    stage="container_inspection",
   101	                    analyzer=self.name,
   102	                    analyzer_version=self.version,
   103	                    category="container",
   104	                    type="CONTAINER_EVASION_DETECTED",
   105	                    title="Container Evasion Detected",
   106	                    description=f"Detected {len(anomalies)} container evasion/corruption anomaly(ies) in {p.name}. Recommendation: use repair_container.",
   107	                    severity=EvidenceSeverity.HIGH,
   108	                    confidence=EvidenceConfidence.HIGH,
   109	                    source=p.name,
   110	                    metadata={"anomalies": anomalies, "zip_valid": zip_valid},
   111	                )
   112	            )
   113	
   114	        summary = (
   115	            f"Container {p.name}: {len(entries)} entries, {len(dex_files)} DEX file(s), "
   116	            f"Manifest: {'Present' if has_manifest else 'Missing'}, Standard ZIP Valid: {zip_valid}. "
   117	            f"Anomalies detected: {len(anomalies)}."
   118	        )
   119	        if anomalies:
   120	            summary += f" [EVASION DETECTED: {'; '.join(anomalies[:3])}. Use repair_container to fix.]"
   121	
   122	        return ToolOutput(
   123	            tool_name=self.name,
   124	            status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS,
   125	            duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   126	            output_summary=summary,
   127	            new_evidence=new_evidence,
   128	            raw_data={
   129	                "total_entries": len(entries),
   130	                "dex_files": dex_files,
   131	                "has_manifest": has_manifest,
   132	                "zip_valid": zip_valid,
   133	                "anomalies": anomalies,
   134	                "zip_error": zip_error,
   135	            },
   136	        )
   137	"""
   138	KAVACH AI 2.0 - APK Container Repair & Evasion Normalization Tool.
   139	Repairs malformed ZIP compression methods (e.g. 1076, 64509), clears fake encryption bits,
   140	and reconstructs central directory entries into a clean derived artifact.
   141	Original APK remains strictly immutable.
   142	"""
   143	
   144	from __future__ import annotations
   145	import os
   146	import io
   147	import time
   148	import zlib
   149	import zipfile
   150	import hashlib
   151	import struct
   152	from pathlib import Path
   153	from typing import Dict, Any, List
   154	
   155	from tools.base import BaseTool, ToolFamily, RepairContainerInput, ToolOutput, ToolExecutionStatus
   156	from tools.base import ArtifactHandleError, artifact_handle_rejection, resolve_artifact_handle
   157	from analyzers.results import ProducedArtifact, ArtifactType
   158	from domain.evidence import Evidence, EvidenceOrigin, EvidenceSeverity, EvidenceConfidence
   159	
   160	
   161	def compute_bytes_sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
   162	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   163	
   164	
   165	class ContainerRepairTool(BaseTool):
   166	    name: str = "repair_container"
   167	    family = ToolFamily.CONTAINER
   168	    version: str = "1.0.0"
   169	    category: str = "reconstruction"
   170	    description: str = "Repairs malformed ZIP headers, strips fake encryption flags, and normalizes container evasion to produce a clean derived APK."
   171	
   172	    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
   173	        return True
   174	
   175	    async def execute(self, tool_input: RepairContainerInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
   176	        start_time = time.time()
   177	        try:
   178	            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
   179	        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
   180	            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)
   181	
   182	        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
   183	            return ToolOutput(
   184	                tool_name=self.name,
   185	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   186	                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   187	                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
   188	                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
   189	            )
   190	
   191	        work_dir = Path(context.get("work_dir", "/tmp"))
   192	        p = Path(artifact_path)
   193	        raw_bytes = p.read_bytes()
   194	
   195	        fixed_entries: List[str] = []
   196	        evidence_list: List[Evidence] = []
   197	        warnings: List[str] = []
   198	
   199	        # Local file header signature: 0x04034b50 ("PK\x03\x04")
   200	        # Format: <4s 5H 3I 2H
   201	        # struct: sig (4), ver (2), flag (2), comp (2), modtime (2), moddate (2), crc (4), csize (4), usize (4), nlen (2), elen (2)
   202	        repaired_buf = bytearray(raw_bytes)
   203	        offset = 0
   204	
   205	        # Pass 1: Scan and repair Local File Headers (PK\x03\x04)
   206	        while offset < len(repaired_buf) - 30:
   207	            if repaired_buf[offset:offset+4] == b"PK\x03\x04":
   208	                header_data = bytes(repaired_buf[offset:offset+30])
   209	                sig, ver, flag, comp, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, nlen, elen = struct.unpack("<4sHHHHHIIIHH", header_data)
   210	                
   211	                fn_start = offset + 30
   212	                fn_end = fn_start + nlen
   213	                filename = bytes(repaired_buf[fn_start:fn_end]).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
   214	
   215	                modified_header = False
   216	
   217	                # 1. Strip fake encryption flag (bit 0)
   218	                if flag & 0x0001:
   219	                    flag &= ~0x0001
   220	                    modified_header = True
   221	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:local_cleared_fake_encryption_bit")
   222	
   223	                # 2. Normalize non-standard compression method (CRC-grounded detection)
   224	                if comp not in (0, 8):
   225	                    data_start = fn_end + elen
   226	                    candidate_stored = bytes(repaired_buf[data_start:data_start + usize])
   227	                    if len(candidate_stored) == usize and usize > 0 and zlib.crc32(candidate_stored) == crc:
   228	                        # Data is actually stored (uncompressed) — CRC matches raw bytes
   229	                        comp = 0
   230	                        csize = usize
   231	                    elif csize > 0:
   232	                        comp = 8
   233	                    else:
   234	                        comp = 0
   235	                        csize = usize
   236	                    modified_header = True
   237	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:local_normalized_comp_{comp}")
   238	
   239	                # 3. Fix zero csize on stored uncompressed files
   240	                if comp == 0 and csize == 0 and usize > 0:
   241	                    csize = usize
   242	                    modified_header = True
   243	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:local_fixed_zero_csize_{usize}")
   244	
   245	                if modified_header:
   246	                    new_hdr = struct.pack("<4sHHHHHIIIHH", sig, ver, flag, comp, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, nlen, elen)
   247	                    repaired_buf[offset:offset+30] = new_hdr
   248	
   249	                offset = fn_end + elen + csize
   250	            else:
   251	                offset += 1
   252	
   253	        # Pass 2: Scan and repair Central Directory Headers (PK\x01\x02)
   254	        offset = 0
   255	        while offset < len(repaired_buf) - 46:
   256	            if repaired_buf[offset:offset+4] == b"PK\x01\x02":
   257	                cd_data = bytes(repaired_buf[offset:offset+46])
   258	                sig, ver_made, ver_need, flag, comp, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, nlen, elen, clen, disk, iattr, eattr, lhdr_off = struct.unpack(
   259	                    "<4sHHHHHHIIIHHHHHII", cd_data
   260	                )
   261	                fn_start = offset + 46
   262	                fn_end = fn_start + nlen
   263	                filename = bytes(repaired_buf[fn_start:fn_end]).decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
   264	
   265	                modified_cd = False
   266	
   267	                if flag & 0x0001:
   268	                    flag &= ~0x0001
   269	                    modified_cd = True
   270	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:cd_cleared_fake_encryption_bit")
   271	
   272	                if comp not in (0, 8):
   273	                    # CRC-grounded detection using local header data at lhdr_off
   274	                    lh_data_start = None
   275	                    try:
   276	                        lh_nlen = struct.unpack_from("<H", repaired_buf, lhdr_off + 26)[0]
   277	                        lh_elen = struct.unpack_from("<H", repaired_buf, lhdr_off + 28)[0]
   278	                        lh_data_start = lhdr_off + 30 + lh_nlen + lh_elen
   279	                    except Exception:
   280	                        pass
   281	                    if lh_data_start is not None and usize > 0:
   282	                        candidate_stored = bytes(repaired_buf[lh_data_start:lh_data_start + usize])
   283	                        if len(candidate_stored) == usize and zlib.crc32(candidate_stored) == crc:
   284	                            comp = 0
   285	                            csize = usize
   286	                        elif csize > 0:
   287	                            comp = 8
   288	                        else:
   289	                            comp = 0
   290	                            csize = usize
   291	                    elif csize > 0:
   292	                        comp = 8
   293	                    else:
   294	                        comp = 0
   295	                        csize = usize
   296	                    modified_cd = True
   297	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:cd_normalized_comp_{comp}")
   298	
   299	                if comp == 0 and csize == 0 and usize > 0:
   300	                    csize = usize
   301	                    modified_cd = True
   302	                    fixed_entries.append(f"{filename}:cd_fixed_zero_csize_{usize}")
   303	
   304	
   305	                if modified_cd:
   306	                    new_cd = struct.pack(
   307	                        "<4sHHHHHHIIIHHHHHII",
   308	                        sig, ver_made, ver_need, flag, comp, mtime, mdate, crc, csize, usize, nlen, elen, clen, disk, iattr, eattr, lhdr_off
   309	                    )
   310	                    repaired_buf[offset:offset+46] = new_cd
   311	
   312	                offset = fn_end + elen + clen
   313	            else:
   314	                offset += 1
   315	
   316	        # Save repaired artifact
   317	        repaired_bytes = bytes(repaired_buf)
   318	        repaired_sha256 = compute_bytes_sha256(repaired_bytes)
   319	        work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   320	        repaired_path = work_dir / f"repaired_{repaired_sha256[:12]}.apk"
   321	        repaired_path.write_bytes(repaired_bytes)
   322	
   323	        # Validate repaired ZIP
   324	        valid_unzip = False
   325	        try:
   326	            with zipfile.ZipFile(repaired_path, "r") as zf:
   327	                bad_file = zf.testzip()
   328	                valid_unzip = (bad_file is None)
   329	        except Exception as ze:
   330	            warnings.append(f"Repaired ZIP validation warning: {ze}")
   331	
   332	        produced_artifacts: List[ProducedArtifact] = [
   333	            ProducedArtifact(
   334	                artifact_type=ArtifactType.ORIGINAL_APK,
   335	                storage_uri=str(repaired_path),
   336	                size_bytes=len(repaired_bytes),
   337	                sha256=repaired_sha256,
   338	                producer=self.name,
   339	                producer_version=self.version,
   340	            )
   341	        ]
   342	
   343	        if fixed_entries:
   344	            evidence_list.append(
   345	                Evidence(
   346	                    run_id=context.get("run_id", "manual"),
   347	                    origin=EvidenceOrigin.RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED,
   348	                    stage="reconstruction",
   349	                    analyzer=self.name,
   350	                    analyzer_version=self.version,
   351	                    category="reconstruction",
   352	                    type="CONTAINER_EVASION_REPAIRED",
   353	                    title="Container Evasion Repaired",
   354	                    description=f"Repaired {len(fixed_entries)} malformed container header(s) in {p.name}. Derived clean artifact: {repaired_path.name}",
   355	                    severity=EvidenceSeverity.HIGH,
   356	                    confidence=EvidenceConfidence.HIGH,
   357	                    source=p.name,
   358	                    normalized_value=repaired_sha256,
   359	                    metadata={"fixed_entries": fixed_entries, "repaired_sha256": repaired_sha256},
   360	                )
   361	            )
   362	
   363	        summary = f"Repaired {len(fixed_entries)} header anomalie(s) in {p.name}. Derived valid ZIP: {valid_unzip} (SHA-256: {repaired_sha256[:12]})"
   364	        return ToolOutput(
   365	            tool_name=self.name,
   366	            status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS if valid_unzip else ToolExecutionStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
   367	            duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   368	            output_summary=summary,
   369	            produced_artifacts=produced_artifacts,
   370	            new_evidence=evidence_list,
   371	            raw_data={
   372	                "original_sha256": compute_bytes_sha256(raw_bytes),
   373	                "repaired_sha256": repaired_sha256,
   374	                "repaired_path": str(repaired_path),
   375	                "fixed_entries": fixed_entries,
   376	                "valid_zip": valid_unzip,
   377	            },
   378	            warnings=warnings,
   379	        )

--- [Step 41 | THINKING] ---
I'm noticing that the repair_container logic assumes any encryption bit is fake and clears it unconditionally, even on genuinely encrypted entries, which would make testzip fail CRC checks on real encrypted data and produce an inconclusive result rather than a clean validation.

But the deeper issue is that "Container Evasion Repaired" evidence with RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED origin gets emitted into the ledger whenever fixed_entries is non-empty, regardless of whether valid_unzip actually succeeded — so even a failed, invalid repair still injects HIGH-severity "verified" evidence and affects the risk score. I'm also realizing the raw byte scan for ZIP local file headers doesn't respect compressed/stored data boundaries properly when data-descriptor entries (bit 3, csize==0) are present, so it can scan into and "repair" bytes inside embedded payloads like nested APKs/JARs, corrupting legitimate content and breaking its CRC.

Beyond correctness, there's a serious performance problem: the central directory scan walks byte-by-byte through the entire file rather than skipping over local entries using their compressed sizes, so a 100MB APK could mean on the order of 100 million synchronous Python iterations — blocking the event loop for tens of seconds despite being declared async. On top of that, the repaired artifact is being mislabeled with the ORIGINAL_APK type, which is misleading since it's actually a modified/reconstructed file.

I'm also noticing the evasion-detection logic is overly aggressive — any parse flag or ZipFile error, even from a benign corrupted download, gets flagged as "Container Evasion Detected" with HIGH severity and HIGH confidence, with no differentiation between legitimate anomalies (zip64, data descriptors) and actual evasion techniques. Walking through the demo fixture scenario, this cascades through triage, inspection, and repair stages all tagging things HIGH when some of these cases are likely benign.

I want to actually run this to see what risk level the report produces, since benign samples should be labeled as such — I'll check whether llama-server and a local model are available to test this properly, or fall back to a mock provider. Then I should move on to search_strings, find_xrefs, decode_static_payload, and jadx for the next phase.

   146	"""
   147	KAVACH AI 2.0 - Method & Class Cross-Reference (XREF) Discovery Tool.
   148	Traces callers, callees, and field accesses across Dalvik bytecode using Androguard analysis graph.
   149	"""
   150	
   151	from __future__ import annotations
   152	import os
   153	import time
   154	from pathlib import Path

   156

   159

   161	class FindXrefsTool(BaseTool):
   162	    name: str = "find_xrefs"
   163	    family = ToolFamily.DISCOVERY
   164	    version: str = "1.0.0"
   165	    category: str = "discovery"
   166	    description: str = "Traces callers and callees for a specified target class or method in Dalvik bytecode."
   167	
   168	    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
   169	        try:
   170	            import androguard
   171	            return True
   172	        except ImportError:
   173	            return False
   174	
   175	    async def execute(self, tool_input: FindXrefsInput, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> ToolOutput:
   176	        start_time = time.time()
   177	        try:
   178	            artifact_path = resolve_artifact_handle(context, tool_input.artifact_id)
   179	        except ArtifactHandleError as e:
   180	            return artifact_handle_rejection(self.name, start_time, e)
   181	
   182	        if not os.path.exists(artifact_path):
   183	            return ToolOutput(
   184	                tool_name=self.name,
   185	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   186	                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   187	                output_summary=f"Artifact not found on disk: {tool_input.artifact_id}",
   188	                error="ARTIFACT_NOT_FOUND",
   189	            )
   190	
   191	        callers: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
   192	        callees: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
   193	
   194	        try:
   195	            from androguard.misc import AnalyzeAPK
   196	            _, _, dx = AnalyzeAPK(str(artifact_path))
   197	
   198	            target_cls = tool_input.target_class
   199	            # Normalize class format (e.g. com.example.Foo -> Lcom/example/Foo;)
   200	            if not target_cls.startswith("L") and not target_cls.endswith(";"):
   201	                norm_cls = f"L{target_cls.replace('.', '/')};"
   202	            else:
   203	                norm_cls = target_cls
   204	
   205	            method_pattern = tool_input.target_method or ".*"
   206	
   207	            for m in dx.find_methods(classname=re_escape_class(norm_cls), methodname=method_pattern):
   208	                # Callers (who calls this method)
   209	                if tool_input.direction in ("callers", "both"):
   210	                    for caller_cls, call, offset in m.get_xref_from():
   211	                        callers.append({
   212	                            "caller_class": str(caller_cls.name),
   213	                            "caller_method": str(call.name),
   214	                            "offset": str(offset),
   215	                        })
   216	
   217	                # Callees (who this method calls)
   218	                if tool_input.direction in ("callees", "both"):
   219	                    for callee_cls, call, offset in m.get_xref_to():
   220	                        callees.append({
   221	                            "callee_class": str(callee_cls.name),
   222	                            "callee_method": str(call.name),
   223	                            "offset": str(offset),
   224	                        })
   225	
   226	            summary = f"Located {len(callers)} callers and {len(callees)} callees for {target_cls}::{method_pattern}"
   227	            return ToolOutput(
   228	                tool_name=self.name,
   229	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS if (callers or callees) else ToolExecutionStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
   230	                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   231	                output_summary=summary,
   232	                raw_data={
   233	                    "target_class": target_cls,
   234	                    "target_method": tool_input.target_method,
   235	                    "callers_count": len(callers),
   236	                    "callees_count": len(callees),
   237	                    "callers": callers[:50],
   238	                    "callees": callees[:50],
   239	                },
   240	            )
   241	
   242	        except Exception as e:
   243	            return ToolOutput(
   244	                tool_name=self.name,
   245	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   246	                duration_seconds=round(time.time() - start_time, 3),
   247	                output_summary=f"XREF analysis failed: {e}",
   248	                error=str(e),
   249	            )
   250	
   251	
   252	def re_escape_class(cls_str: str) -> str:
   253	    return cls_str.replace("$", "\\$")

--- [Step 43 | THINKING] ---
This is a crucial finding: search_strings only exposes counts to the model via output_summary, never the actual matched strings or contexts from raw_data. The same blindness applies to find_xrefs — the model only sees counts, not actual callers/callees, since raw_data is never projected through to_observation. So the agent's investigation is effectively blind, operating only on counting oracles rather than real content.

Beyond this visibility gap, I'm spotting serious security issues: search_strings runs model-supplied regex with IGNORECASE over potentially the entire decoded DEX text, and Python's re has no timeout — a catastrophic backtracking pattern could hang the whole service synchronously on the event loop thread, since the execution is blocking despite being wrapped in async. There's also an unbounded decompression risk: zf.read(name) has no size cap, so a malicious zip entry claiming a huge size could trigger an OOM kill, and search_strings over a zip reads every matching entry fully without any bound.

I'm also noting find_xrefs runs AnalyzeAPK on the whole APK in-process per call, which is slow and memory-heavy, and it passes model-supplied regex (via method/class patterns) straight into androguard's matching functions, creating another ReDoS surface — the class name escaping only handles `$`, leaving other regex metacharacters exploitable. Error messages are sanitized at projection, so that's handled. Now I'm moving on to look at payload decoding, jadx, and ELF handling.

    14	from typing import Dict, Any, List

    16	from tools.base import BaseTool, ToolFamily, DecodeStaticPayloadInput, ToolOutput, ToolExecutionStatus

    21	
    22	def compute_bytes_sha256(data: bytes) -> str:
    23	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    24	
    25	
    26	def carve_dex_from_stream(data: bytes, offset: int = 0) -> List[bytes]:
    27	    """Scans stream for dex\\n03X magic and carves full DEX based on header file_size."""
    28	    carved_dex_list: List[bytes] = []
    29	    idx = offset
    30	    while idx < len(data) - 40:
    31	        chunk = data[idx:idx+8]
    32	        if chunk.startswith(b"dex\n03"):
    33	            # In DEX header, offset 32 (0x20) is 4-byte uint32 file_size
    34	            if idx + 36 <= len(data):
    35	                file_size = struct.unpack("<I", data[idx+32:idx+36])[0]
    36	                if 40 <= file_size <= (len(data) - idx):
    37	                    dex_bytes = data[idx:idx+file_size]
    38	                    carved_dex_list.append(dex_bytes)
    39	                    idx += file_size
    40	                    continue
    41	        idx += 1
    42	    return carved_dex_list
    43	
    44	
    45	def xor_transform(data: bytes, key: bytes) -> bytes:
    46	    if not key:
    47	        return data
    48	    k_len = len(key)
    49	    return bytes(b ^ key[i % k_len] for i, b in enumerate(data))
    50	
    51	
    52	def rc4_transform(data: bytes, key: bytes) -> bytes:
    53	    """Standard RC4 stream cipher transformation."""
    54	    S = list(range(256))
    55	    j = 0
    56	    for i in range(256):
    57	        j = (j + S[i] + key[i % len(key)]) % 256
    58	        S[i], S[j] = S[j], S[i]
    59	
    60	    i = j = 0
    61	    res = bytearray()
    62	    for byte in data:
    63	        i = (i + 1) % 256
    64	        j = (j + S[i]) % 256
    65	        S[i], S[j] = S[j], S[i]
    66	        k = S[(S[i] + S[j]) % 256]
    67	        res.append(byte ^ k)
    68	    return bytes(res)
    69

   331	            provenance = execution_provenance(result)

   340	                )

   344	            java_files = list(sources_dir.rglob("*.java")) if sources_dir.exists() else []
   345

   351	                        storage_uri=str(sources_dir),

   353	                        producer=self.name,
   354	                        producer_version=self.version,
   355	                        metadata={"execution_id": result.execution_id, "parent_artifact_id": tool_input.artifact_id},
   356	                    )
   357	                )

   359	            summary = f"JADX decompiled {len(java_files)} Java classes from {tool_input.artifact_id}"
   360	            return ToolOutput(

   362	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS if java_files else ToolExecutionStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,

   371	                },
   372	            )
   373	
   374	        except Exception as e:

   377	                status=ToolExecutionStatus.FAILED,

   380	                error=str(e),
   381	            )

--- [Step 45 | THINKING] ---
I'm reconsidering whether raw dex carving would actually work on the original APK, since classes.dex is normally deflate-compressed inside the zip -- it'd only be scannable in raw form if the dex were stored uncompressed, which some newer Android builds allow.

This exposes a false-positive path: any legitimate app with high-entropy assets like JPGs or fonts in assets/ could trigger a HIDDEN_PAYLOAD hypothesis, and then carving the ordinary stored classes.dex would get misread as confirming it,
