[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
     1	"""
     2	KAVACH AI 2.0 - ExecutionBroker Central Dispatcher.
     3	Validates ExecutionRequests, evaluates security policies, resolves concrete backends,
     4	and executes requests under strict non-downgradable isolation guarantees.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	import uuid
     9	import logging
    10	import datetime
    11	from typing import Optional, Dict, Any
    12	
    13	from execution.models import (
    14	    IsolationTier,
    15	    ExecutionStatus,
    16	    ExecutionRequest,
    17	    ExecutionResult,
    18	)
    19	from execution.policy import ExecutionPolicy, ResolvedExecutionPolicy
    20	from execution.resolver import BackendResolver
    21	
    22	logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.execution.broker")
    23	
    24	
    25	class ExecutionBroker:
    26	    """
    27	    Central dispatcher coordinating forensic tool execution across isolated environments.
    28	    Guarantees:
    29	    1. Validation: All requests undergo strict schema and argument checks.
    30	    2. Policy Authority: Agent requests are evaluated; isolation is escalated when needed,
    31	       and never downgraded. Network/filesystem requests are neutralized where unapproved.
    32	    3. Fail Closed: High-risk CRITICAL requests fail closed if high-assurance backends
    33	       are absent. Never silently falls back to Bwrap.
    34	    """
    35	
    36	    def __init__(
    37	        self,
    38	        policy: Optional[ExecutionPolicy] = None,
    39	        resolver: Optional[BackendResolver] = None,
    40	    ):
    41	        self.policy = policy or ExecutionPolicy()
    42	        self.resolver = resolver or BackendResolver()
    43	
    44	    async def execute(self, request: ExecutionRequest) -> ExecutionResult:
    45	        """
    46	        Execute an ExecutionRequest through policy resolution, backend selection,
    47	        and isolated execution.
    48	        """
    49	        start_dt = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
    50	        start_iso = start_dt.isoformat()
    51	        exec_id = request.request_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    52	
    53	        # 1. Basic Request Validation
    54	        if request.code is None and not request.command and not request.arguments:
    55	            return ExecutionResult(
    56	                execution_id=exec_id,
    57	                status=ExecutionStatus.POLICY_REJECTED,
    58	                exit_code=-1,
    59	                requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
    60	                policy_required_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
    61	                effective_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
    62	                backend_id="none",
    63	                timestamps={"started_at": start_iso, "completed_at": start_iso},
    64	                warnings=["Request validation failure: neither code nor command provided."],
    65	                error="Execution request must provide either 'code' for Python execution or 'command'/'arguments' for shell execution.",
    66	            )
    67	
    68	        # 2. Policy Evaluation & Tier Escalation
    69	        resolved_policy: ResolvedExecutionPolicy = self.policy.resolve(request)
    70	        effective_tier = resolved_policy.effective_isolation
    71	
    72	        # 3. Fail-Closed Check for CRITICAL Tier
    73	        if effective_tier == IsolationTier.CRITICAL:
    74	            backend = await self.resolver.resolve_backend(effective_tier)
    75	            if backend is None:
    76	                err_msg = (
    77	                    "CRITICAL isolation tier required by policy, but no compliant "
    78	                    "high-assurance backend (microVM/hypervisor) is registered or available. "
    79	                    "Failing closed without downgrade to bwrap."
    80	                )
    81	                logger.error(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] {err_msg}")
    82	                return ExecutionResult(
    83	                    execution_id=exec_id,
    84	                    status=ExecutionStatus.POLICY_REJECTED,
    85	                    exit_code=-1,
    86	                    requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
    87	                    policy_required_isolation=resolved_policy.policy_required_isolation,
    88	                    effective_isolation=effective_tier,
    89	                    backend_id="none",
    90	                    timestamps={"started_at": start_iso, "completed_at": start_iso},
    91	                    warnings=resolved_policy.warnings,
    92	                    error=err_msg,
    93	                )
    94	        else:
    95	            backend = await self.resolver.resolve_backend(effective_tier)
    96	
    97	        # 4. General Backend Availability Resolution
    98	        if backend is None:
    99	            err_msg = f"No available execution backend satisfies isolation tier '{effective_tier.value}'."
   100	            logger.error(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] {err_msg}")
   101	            return ExecutionResult(
   102	                execution_id=exec_id,
   103	                status=ExecutionStatus.POLICY_REJECTED,
   104	                exit_code=-1,
   105	                requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
   106	                policy_required_isolation=resolved_policy.policy_required_isolation,
   107	                effective_isolation=effective_tier,
   108	                backend_id="none",
   109	                timestamps={"started_at": start_iso, "completed_at": start_iso},
   110	                warnings=resolved_policy.warnings,
   111	                error=err_msg,
   112	            )
   113	
   114	        # 5. Dispatch Execution to Selected Backend
   115	        try:
   116	            logger.info(
   117	                f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Routing '{request.tool_id}' to backend '{backend.backend_id}' "
   118	                f"(effective tier: {effective_tier.value}, requested: {request.requested_isolation.value})"
   119	            )
   120	            result = await backend.execute(request, resolved_policy)
   121	            return result
   122	
   123	        except Exception as exc:
   124	            end_iso = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat()
   125	            logger.exception(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Backend execution error: {exc}")
   126	            return ExecutionResult(
   127	                execution_id=exec_id,
   128	                status=ExecutionStatus.FAILED,
   129	                exit_code=-1,
   130	                requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
   131	                policy_required_isolation=resolved_policy.policy_required_isolation,
   132	                effective_isolation=effective_tier,
   133	                backend_id=backend.backend_id,
   134	                timestamps={"started_at": start_iso, "completed_at": end_iso},
   135	                warnings=resolved_policy.warnings,
   136	                error=f"Backend execution failure: {str(exc)}",
   137	            )
   138	"""
   139	KAVACH AI 2.0 - ExecutionPolicy & Security Governor.
   140	Enforces tier escalation, non-downgradable sandbox constraints,
   141	request authority neutralization, and fail-closed security invariants.
   142	"""
   143	
   144	from __future__ import annotations
   145	import logging
   146	from typing import Dict, List, Any, Optional
   147	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
   148	
   149	from execution.models import IsolationTier, ExecutionRequest
   150	
   151	logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.execution.policy")
   152	
   153	
   154	# ============================================================================
   155	# 1. Tool Required Isolation Registry
   156	# ============================================================================
   157	
   158	TOOL_MINIMUM_ISOLATION: Dict[str, IsolationTier] = {
   159	    # Lightweight static inspection
   160	    "read_file": IsolationTier.LOW,
   161	    "inspect_artifact": IsolationTier.LOW,
   162	    "list_directory": IsolationTier.LOW,
   163	    "discover_capabilities": IsolationTier.LOW,
   164	    "discover_tools": IsolationTier.LOW,
   165	    "get_tool_schema": IsolationTier.LOW,
   166	    "inspect_state": IsolationTier.LOW,
   167	
   168	    # File generation and artifact creation in controlled work directories
   169	    "write_file": IsolationTier.MEDIUM,
   170	    "create_derived_artifact": IsolationTier.MEDIUM,
   171	    "repair_container": IsolationTier.MEDIUM,
   172	    "decode_static_payload": IsolationTier.MEDIUM,
   173	
   174	    # Out-of-process subprocess execution
   175	    "python_exec": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   176	    "shell_exec": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   177	    "run_jadx": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   178	    "inspect_elf_symbols": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   179	    "search_strings": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   180	    "find_payload_candidates": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   181	    "find_xrefs": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   182	    "inspect_container": IsolationTier.HIGH,
   183	
   184	    # Dangerous dynamic operations requiring dedicated high-assurance microVM
   185	    "live_malware_detonation": IsolationTier.CRITICAL,
   186	    "untrusted_dynamic_exec": IsolationTier.CRITICAL,
   187	    "kernel_probe": IsolationTier.CRITICAL,
   188	    "root_capability": IsolationTier.CRITICAL,
   189	    "frida_dynamic_trace": IsolationTier.CRITICAL,
   190	}
   191	
   192	# Network allowlist: tools explicitly permitted network egress (empty by default)
   193	NETWORK_ALLOWED_TOOLS: set[str] = set()
   194	
   195	
   196	# ============================================================================
   197	# 2. Resolved Execution Policy Model
   198	# ============================================================================
   199	
   200	class ResolvedExecutionPolicy(BaseModel):
   201	    """
   202	    Immutable specification of effective execution parameters resolved by ExecutionPolicy.
   203	    Agent request values have been evaluated, clamped, or overridden by policy authority.
   204	    """
   205	    tool_id: str
   206	    requested_isolation: IsolationTier
   207	    policy_required_isolation: IsolationTier
   208	    effective_isolation: IsolationTier
   209	    effective_network_policy: str = "NETWORK_NONE"
   210	    effective_filesystem_policy: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
   211	    effective_resource_class: str = "STANDARD"
   212	    effective_timeout_seconds: float = 30.0
   213	    requires_high_assurance: bool = False
   214	    warnings: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
   215	
   216	
   217	# ============================================================================
   218	# 3. Execution Policy Evaluator
   219	# ============================================================================
   220	
   221	class ExecutionPolicy:
   222	    """
   223	    Evaluates ExecutionRequests against system invariants:
   224	    1. Tier Escalation: policy may escalate isolation; agent can NEVER downgrade.
   225	    2. Request Neutralization: filesystem, network, and resource settings from the agent
   226	       are requests, not authority.
   227	    3. Fail Closed: CRITICAL operations require verified high-assurance isolation.
   228	    """
   229	
   230	    def __init__(
   231	        self,
   232	        default_required_tier: IsolationTier = IsolationTier.HIGH,
   233	        max_timeout_seconds: float = 300.0,
   234	        enforce_no_network: bool = True,
   235	    ):
   236	        self.default_required_tier = default_required_tier
   237	        self.max_timeout_seconds = max_timeout_seconds
   238	        self.enforce_no_network = enforce_no_network
   239	
   240	    def resolve(self, request: ExecutionRequest) -> ResolvedExecutionPolicy:
   241	        """
   242	        Evaluate and resolve an ExecutionRequest into a concrete ResolvedExecutionPolicy.
   243	        """
   244	        warnings: List[str] = []
   245	
   246	        # 1. Determine Policy Required Isolation Tier
   247	        policy_required = TOOL_MINIMUM_ISOLATION.get(request.tool_id, self.default_required_tier)
   248	
   249	        # 2. Tier Escalation Invariant:
   250	        # Effective tier is max(requested, policy_required).
   251	        # Agent can elevate to higher isolation, but cannot downgrade below policy_required.
   252	        if request.requested_isolation < policy_required:
   253	            effective_tier = policy_required
   254	            warnings.append(
   255	                f"Requested isolation tier {request.requested_isolation.value} escalated to "
   256	                f"{policy_required.value} by execution policy for tool '{request.tool_id}'."
   257	            )
   258	        else:
   259	            effective_tier = request.requested_isolation
   260	
   261	        # 3. Network Policy Authority:
   262	        # Agent network requests are not authority. Unless tool is in NETWORK_ALLOWED_TOOLS,
   263	        # network access is denied (clamped to NETWORK_NONE).
   264	        effective_network = "NETWORK_NONE"
   265	        if request.network_policy and request.network_policy != "NETWORK_NONE":
   266	            if request.tool_id not in NETWORK_ALLOWED_TOOLS or self.enforce_no_network:
   267	                warnings.append(
   268	                    f"Agent requested network policy '{request.network_policy}' denied by policy; "
   269	                    "forced to NETWORK_NONE."
   270	                )
   271	                effective_network = "NETWORK_NONE"
   272	            else:
   273	                effective_network = request.network_policy
   274	
   275	        # 4. Filesystem Policy Authority:
   276	        # Prevent unauthorized write requests or escaping beyond forensic domain.
   277	        effective_fs: Dict[str, Any] = {
   278	            "cwd_uri": request.cwd_uri,
   279	            "read_domains": ["input", "forensic", "artifact"],
   280	            "write_domains": ["forensic", "artifact"],
   281	        }
   282	        if request.cwd_uri.startswith("input://"):
   283	            # Writes inside input:// are strictly prohibited
   284	            warnings.append("Requested working directory inside input:// domain; clamped to forensic://work.")
   285	            effective_fs["cwd_uri"] = "forensic://work"
   286	
   287	        # 5. Resource Limits Authority:
   288	        # Clamp timeout to max_timeout_seconds
   289	        effective_timeout = min(request.timeout_seconds, self.max_timeout_seconds)
   290	        if request.timeout_seconds > self.max_timeout_seconds:
   291	            warnings.append(
   292	                f"Requested timeout {request.timeout_seconds}s exceeds ceiling {self.max_timeout_seconds}s; "
   293	                f"clamped to {effective_timeout}s."
   294	            )
   295	
   296	        effective_res_class = request.resource_class or "STANDARD"
   297	
   298	        requires_high_assurance = (effective_tier == IsolationTier.CRITICAL)
   299	
   300	        return ResolvedExecutionPolicy(
   301	            tool_id=request.tool_id,
   302	            requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
   303	            policy_required_isolation=policy_required,
   304	            effective_isolation=effective_tier,
   305	            effective_network_policy=effective_network,
   306	            effective_filesystem_policy=effective_fs,
   307	            effective_resource_class=effective_res_class,
   308	            effective_timeout_seconds=effective_timeout,
   309	            requires_high_assurance=requires_high_assurance,
   310	            warnings=warnings,
   311	        )
   312	"""
   313	KAVACH AI 2.0 - BackendResolver.
   314	Resolves concrete execution backend from resolved IsolationTier and runtime availability.
   315	Enforces fail-closed isolation security: CRITICAL requests never downgrade to Bwrap.
   316	"""
   317	
   318	from __future__ import annotations
   319	import logging
   320	from typing import Dict, Optional, Set
   321	from execution.models import IsolationTier
   322	from execution.backends.base import BaseExecutionBackend
   323	
   324	logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.execution.resolver")
   325	
   326	
   327	class NoAvailableBackendError(RuntimeError):
   328	    """Raised when no registered backend satisfies the required isolation tier."""
   329	    pass
   330	
   331	
   332	class BackendResolver:
   333	    """
   334	    Manages registered execution backends and maps effective IsolationTiers to
   335	    concrete backends based on host availability and security policy.
   336	    """
   337	
   338	    def __init__(self, backends: Optional[Dict[str, BaseExecutionBackend]] = None):
   339	        self._backends: Dict[str, BaseExecutionBackend] = dict(backends or {})
   340	
   341	    def register_backend(self, backend: BaseExecutionBackend) -> None:
   342	        """Register a new backend instance."""
   343	        self._backends[backend.backend_id] = backend
   344	
   345	    def get_backend(self, backend_id: str) -> Optional[BaseExecutionBackend]:
   346	        """Direct retrieval by backend identifier."""
   347	        return self._backends.get(backend_id)
   348	
   349	    async def resolve_backend(self, tier: IsolationTier) -> Optional[BaseExecutionBackend]:
   350	        """
   351	        Resolve an available execution backend that satisfies the requested IsolationTier.
   352	
   353	        Security Invariant:
   354	        - If tier is CRITICAL, and no backend declares CRITICAL support, return None (or fail closed).
   355	          NEVER fall back to bwrap or a lower-assurance backend.
   356	        """
   357	        for backend_id, backend in self._backends.items():
   358	            if tier in backend.supported_tiers:
   359	                if await backend.is_available():
   360	                    return backend
   361	                else:
   362	                    logger.warning(f"Backend '{backend_id}' supports tier '{tier.value}' but reported unavailable.")
   363	
   364	        # No backend found that legitimately supports this tier
   365	        if tier == IsolationTier.CRITICAL:
   366	            logger.error(
   367	                "CRITICAL isolation tier required, but no compliant high-assurance backend "
   368	                "(microVM/hypervisor) is registered or available. Failing closed without downgrade."
   369	            )
   370	        return None
   371	
   372	    def list_supported_tiers(self) -> Set[IsolationTier]:
   373	        """Return the union of all isolation tiers supported across registered backends."""
   374	        tiers: Set[IsolationTier] = set()
   375	        for backend in self._backends.values():
   376	            tiers.update(backend.supported_tiers)
   377	        return tiers
   378	"""
   379	KAVACH AI 2.0 - Bubblewrap Workbench Execution Backend.
   380	Wraps WorkbenchExecutor to execute ExecutionRequests inside out-of-process
   381	bubblewrap namespaces, RLIMIT constraints, and three-domain filesystem bounds.
   382	"""
   383	
   384	from __future__ import annotations
   385	import os
   386	import time
   387	import uuid
   388	import hashlib
   389	import datetime
   390	from pathlib import Path
   391	from typing import Set, Dict, Any, Optional
   392	
   393	from execution.models import (
   394	    IsolationTier,
   395	    ExecutionStatus,
   396	    ExecutionRequest,
   397	    ExecutionManifest,
   398	    ExecutionResult,
   399	)
   400	from execution.policy import ResolvedExecutionPolicy
   401	from execution.backends.base import BaseExecutionBackend
   402	from workbench.sandbox import WorkbenchExecutor, ExecutionResult as SandboxResult
   403	from workbench.policy import IsolationProfile, ResourceClass, DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES
   404	
   405	
   406	class BwrapBackend(BaseExecutionBackend):
   407	    """
   408	    Concrete backend wrapping WorkbenchExecutor.
   409	    Supports LOW, MEDIUM, and HIGH isolation tiers.
   410	    Never satisfies CRITICAL isolation tier.
   411	    """
   412	    backend_id: str = "bwrap"
   413	    supported_tiers: Set[IsolationTier] = {
   414	        IsolationTier.LOW,
   415	        IsolationTier.MEDIUM,
   416	        IsolationTier.HIGH,
   417	    }
   418	
   419	    def __init__(self, executor: WorkbenchExecutor):
   420	        self.executor = executor
   421	
   422	    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
   423	        """Available if the underlying WorkbenchExecutor is initialized."""
   424	        return True
   425	
   426	    def _map_isolation_profile(self, tier: IsolationTier) -> IsolationProfile:
   427	        """Map IsolationTier to workbench sandbox IsolationProfile."""
   428	        if tier == IsolationTier.LOW and self.executor.policy.allow_normal_profile:
   429	            return IsolationProfile.NORMAL
   430	        # MEDIUM and HIGH use HARDENED (bwrap namespaces + unshare-all + unshare-net)
   431	        return IsolationProfile.HARDENED
   432	
   433	    def _map_resource_class(self, res_class_str: str) -> ResourceClass:
   434	        """Map resource class string to ResourceClass enum."""
   435	        try:
   436	            return ResourceClass(res_class_str)
   437	        except Exception:
   438	            return ResourceClass.STANDARD
   439	
   440	    def _compute_input_hashes(self, input_artifacts: list[str]) -> Dict[str, str]:
   441	        """Compute SHA256 hashes of input artifacts where accessible on disk."""
   442	        hashes: Dict[str, str] = {}
   443	        for art_ref in input_artifacts:
   444	            try:
   445	                # Check if it's a known artifact ID in resolver
   446	                art_path = self.executor.path_resolver.get_artifact_path(art_ref)
   447	                if art_path and art_path.exists():
   448	                    hashes[art_ref] = hashlib.sha256(art_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
   449	                    continue
   450	                # Check if it's a direct file path or URI
   451	                if os.path.exists(art_ref):
   452	                    hashes[art_ref] = hashlib.sha256(Path(art_ref).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
   453	            except Exception:
   454	                pass
   455	        return hashes
   456	
   457	    async def execute(
   458	        self,
   459	        request: ExecutionRequest,
   460	        resolved_policy: ResolvedExecutionPolicy,
   461	    ) -> ExecutionResult:
   462	        """Execute request via WorkbenchExecutor and return normalized ExecutionResult."""
   463	        start_dt = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
   464	        start_iso = start_dt.isoformat()
   465	
   466	        exec_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
   467	        profile = self._map_isolation_profile(resolved_policy.effective_isolation)
   468	        res_class = self._map_resource_class(resolved_policy.effective_resource_class)
   469	        timeout = resolved_policy.effective_timeout_seconds
   470	        cwd_uri = resolved_policy.effective_filesystem_policy.get("cwd_uri", request.cwd_uri)
   471	
   472	        # 1. Execute Code or Command
   473	        sandbox_res: SandboxResult
   474	        argv_for_manifest: list[str] = []
   475	
   476	        if request.code is not None:
   477	            argv_for_manifest = ["python3", "-c", "<inline_script>"]
   478	            sandbox_res = await self.executor.run_python(
   479	                code=request.code,
   480	                cwd_uri=cwd_uri,
   481	                timeout=timeout,
   482	                isolation_profile=profile,
   483	                resource_class=res_class,
   484	            )
   485	        else:
   486	            cmd = request.command or request.arguments or []
   487	            argv_for_manifest = list(cmd)
   488	            sandbox_res = await self.executor.run_command(
   489	                cmd=cmd,
   490	                cwd_uri=cwd_uri,
   491	                timeout=timeout,
   492	                isolation_profile=profile,
   493	                resource_class=res_class,
   494	            )
   495	
   496	        end_dt = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
   497	        end_iso = end_dt.isoformat()
   498	
   499	        # 2. Map Status
   500	        if sandbox_res.status == "timeout":
   501	            status = ExecutionStatus.TIMEOUT
   502	        elif sandbox_res.quota_exceeded:
   503	            status = ExecutionStatus.OOM if "QUOTA" in (sandbox_res.quota_reason or "") else ExecutionStatus.FAILED
   504	        elif sandbox_res.status == "completed" and sandbox_res.exit_code == 0:
   505	            status = ExecutionStatus.SUCCESS
   506	        elif sandbox_res.status == "cancelled":
   507	            status = ExecutionStatus.CANCELLED
   508	        else:
   509	            status = ExecutionStatus.FAILED
   510	
   511	        # 3. Build Provenance Manifest
   512	        limits = DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES.get(res_class, DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES[ResourceClass.STANDARD])
   513	        input_hashes = self._compute_input_hashes(request.input_artifacts)
   514	
   515	        manifest = ExecutionManifest(
   516	            execution_id=exec_id,
   517	            tool_id=request.tool_id,
   518	            tool_version=request.tool_version,
   519	            command_argv=argv_for_manifest,
   520	            requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
   521	            policy_required_isolation=resolved_policy.policy_required_isolation,
   522	            effective_isolation=resolved_policy.effective_isolation,
   523	            backend_id=self.backend_id,
   524	            input_hashes=input_hashes,
   525	            filesystem_rules=resolved_policy.effective_filesystem_policy,
   526	            network_policy=resolved_policy.effective_network_policy,
   527	            resource_envelope=limits.to_dict(),
   528	            environment_digest=sandbox_res.executable_sha256,
   529	            timestamp_iso=start_iso,
   530	        )
   531	
   532	        # 4. Consolidate Warnings
   533	        all_warnings = list(resolved_policy.warnings)
   534	        if sandbox_res.warnings:
   535	            all_warnings.extend(sandbox_res.warnings)
   536	
   537	        # 5. Output Artifacts
   538	        output_artifacts: list[str] = list(sandbox_res.produced_artifacts)
   539	        if sandbox_res.full_output_artifact_id and sandbox_res.full_output_artifact_id not in output_artifacts:
   540	            output_artifacts.append(sandbox_res.full_output_artifact_id)
   541	
   542	        metrics = {
   543	            "duration_ms": sandbox_res.duration_ms,
   544	            "stdout_bytes": sandbox_res.stdout_bytes,
   545	            "stderr_bytes": sandbox_res.stderr_bytes,
   546	            "resource_usage": sandbox_res.resource_usage,
   547	            "backend": self.backend_id,
   548	        }
   549	
   550	        timestamps = {
   551	            "started_at": start_iso,
   552	            "completed_at": end_iso,
   553	        }
   554	
   555	        error_msg = sandbox_res.stderr if status == ExecutionStatus.FAILED else None
   556	        if sandbox_res.status == "timeout":
   557	            error_msg = f"Execution timed out after {timeout} seconds"
   558	
   559	        return ExecutionResult(
   560	            execution_id=exec_id,
   561	            status=status,
   562	            exit_code=sandbox_res.exit_code,
   563	            stdout=sandbox_res.stdout,
   564	            stderr=sandbox_res.stderr,
   565	            stdout_ref=sandbox_res.stdout_artifact_id,
   566	            stderr_ref=sandbox_res.stderr_artifact_id,
   567	            output_artifacts=output_artifacts,
   568	            requested_isolation=request.requested_isolation,
   569	            policy_required_isolation=resolved_policy.policy_required_isolation,
   570	            effective_isolation=resolved_policy.effective_isolation,
   571	            backend_id=self.backend_id,
   572	            metrics=metrics,
   573	            execution_manifest=manifest.model_dump(),
   574	            timestamps=timestamps,
   575	            warnings=all_warnings,
   576	            error=error_msg,
   577	        )
   578	"""
   579	KAVACH AI 2.0 - Base Execution Backend Protocol.
   580	Abstract contract for out-of-process isolation backends.
   581	"""
   582	
   583	from __future__ import annotations
   584	import abc
   585	from typing import Set
   586	from execution.models import IsolationTier, ExecutionRequest, ExecutionResult
   587	from execution.policy import ResolvedExecutionPolicy
   588	
   589	
   590	class BaseExecutionBackend(abc.ABC):
   591	    """
   592	    Abstract isolation backend capable of executing ExecutionRequests
   593	    under specific IsolationTiers.
   594	    """
   595	    backend_id: str
   596	    supported_tiers: Set[IsolationTier]
   597	
   598	    @abc.abstractmethod
   599	    async def is_available(self) -> bool:
   600	        """Verify host capabilities and dependencies required for this backend."""
   601	        raise NotImplementedError
   602	
   603	    @abc.abstractmethod
   604	    async def execute(
   605	        self,
   606	        request: ExecutionRequest,
   607	        resolved_policy: ResolvedExecutionPolicy,
   608	    ) -> ExecutionResult:
   609	        """Execute request under resolved policy constraints and return ExecutionResult."""
   610	        raise NotImplementedError

--- [Step 48 | THINKING] ---
I notice the policy table claims search_strings, find_xrefs, and inspect_container require HIGH isolation for subprocess execution, but these actually run in-process and never touch the broker — so that policy entry is essentially decorative or misleading, even though the execution record honestly reports NONE/NONE for them.

The effective_isolation field appears to report the resolved tier rather than what was actually measured — I need to check whether BwrapBackend.is_available always returns true and how isolation profiles map, especially whether jadx falls back to unsandboxed execution when bwrap isn't available. Also noting that environment_digest hashes only the jadx launcher script (not the JAR or JVM), timeout is 45s, and I need to dig into sandbox.py's run_command and bwrap fallback logic to resolve the requested-vs-effective isolation question.

    def __init__(
        self,
        path_resolver: PathResolver,
        policy: Optional[WorkbenchPolicy] = None,
        default_timeout: float = 30.0,
        uid: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.path_resolver = path_resolver
        self.policy = policy or WorkbenchPolicy(default_timeout_seconds=default_timeout)
        self.default_timeout = default_timeout
        self.uid = uid
        if self.policy.bwrap_capability and self.policy.bwrap_capability.available:
            self.bwrap_path = self.policy.bwrap_capability.canonical_path
        else:
            self.bwrap_path = shutil.which("bwrap")
        self.python_bin = sys.executable or "/usr/bin/python3"

    @property
    def bwrap_available(self) -> bool:
        return bool(self.policy.bwrap_capability and self.policy.bwrap_capability.available)

    def build_sanitized_environment(self, custom_env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Produce a clean minimal environment without host credentials, secrets, or poisoning vectors."""
        clean_path = "/opt/kavach/runtime/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin"
        # Add virtualenv bin if running in virtualenv
        venv_bin = os.path.dirname(self.python_bin)
        if os.path.exists(venv_bin) and venv_bin not in clean_path:
            clean_path = f"{venv_bin}:{clean_path}"

        project_backend = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
        python_paths = [project_backend]
        for p in sys.path:
            if ("site-packages" in p or "dist-packages" in p) and os.path.exists(p):
                if p not in python_paths:
                    python_paths.append(p)

        env: Dict[str, str] = {
            "PATH": clean_path,
            "PYTHONPATH": ":".join(python_paths),
            "PYTHONUNBUFFERED": "1",
            "LANG": "C.UTF-8",
            "LC_ALL": "C.UTF-8",
            "HOME": str(self.path_resolver.forensic_root),
            "KAVACH_INPUT_ROOT": str(self.path_resolver.input_root),
            "KAVACH_FORENSIC_ROOT": str(self.path_resolver.forensic_root),
            "KAVACH_REPAIR_ROOT": str(self.path_resolver.repair_root),
            "KAVACH_ARTIFACT_ROOT": str(self.path_resolver.artifact_root),
            "KAVACH_ALLOW_REPAIR_WORKSPACE": "1" if self.path_resolver.allow_repair_workspace else "0",
        }

        # Selectively permit non-sensitive, non-poisoning custom vars
        if custom_env:
            for k, v in custom_env.items():
                k_upper = k.upper()
                if k_upper in self.DANGEROUS_ENV_VARS or k_upper.startswith("GIT_") or k_upper.startswith("LD_"):
                    logger.warning(f"Sanitizer stripped dangerous environment variable: {k}")
                    continue
                if any(k_upper.startswith(prefix) for prefix in self.SENSITIVE_ENV_PREFIXES):
                    logger.warning(f"Sanitizer stripped sensitive environment key: {k}")
                    continue
                val_str = str(v)
                if any(val_str.startswith(pfx) for pfx in ("AIza", "ghp_", "gho_", "sk-", "bearer ")):
                    logger.warning(f"Sanitizer stripped credential value in: {k}")
                    continue
                env[k] = val_str

        return env

    def _build_rlimit_preexec(self, timeout_sec: float, resource_class: Optional[ResourceClass] = None):
        """Builds preexec function configuring process group and Linux rlimits derived from ResourceClass."""
        import resource
        rc = resource_class or ResourceClass.STANDARD
        limits = DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES.get(rc, DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES[ResourceClass.STANDARD])

        # Measure base thread count for current UID if not switching to dedicated UID
        base_threads = 0
        if self.uid is None or self.uid == os.getuid():
            try:
                uid = os.getuid()
                for pid in os.listdir("/proc"):
                    if pid.isdigit():
                        try:
                            st = os.stat(f"/proc/{pid}")
                            if st.st_uid == uid:
                                base_threads += len(os.listdir(f"/proc/{pid}/task"))
                        except (OSError, FileNotFoundError):
                            pass
            except Exception:
                base_threads = 0

        def _preexec():
            # Change UID if non-root UID requested and running as root
            if self.uid is not None and os.getuid() == 0:
                try:
                    os.setuid(self.uid)
                except Exception as ue:
                    logger.error(f"Failed to switch to UID {self.uid}: {ue}")

            # Linux RLIMIT limits
            try:
                # CPU time limit in seconds
                cpu_limit = max(int(timeout_sec) + 5, limits.cpu_seconds)
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CPU, (cpu_limit, cpu_limit + 5))
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                # Dynamic address space limit derived from ResourceClass
                as_limit = limits.as_bytes
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS, (as_limit, as_limit))
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                # Concurrently clamp NPROC respecting hard limit and UID baseline
                cur_soft, cur_hard = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NPROC)
                target_soft = base_threads + limits.nproc
                target_hard = base_threads + limits.nproc * 2
                new_soft = target_soft if cur_soft == resource.RLIM_INFINITY else min(cur_soft, target_soft)
                new_hard = target_hard if cur_hard == resource.RLIM_INFINITY else min(cur_hard, target_hard)
                new_hard = max(new_hard, new_soft)
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NPROC, (new_soft, new_hard))
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                # File write size limit derived from ResourceClass
                fsize_limit = limits.fsize_bytes
                cur_fsoft, cur_fhard = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE)
                new_fsoft = fsize_limit if cur_fsoft == resource.RLIM_INFINITY else min(cur_fsoft, fsize_limit)
                new_fhard = cur_fhard if cur_fhard != resource.RLIM_INFINITY else fsize_limit
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_FSIZE, (new_fsoft, new_fhard))
            except Exception:
                pass

            try:
                # File descriptors limit derived from ResourceClass
                cur_nsoft, cur_nhard = resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NOFILE)
                new_nsoft = limits.nofile if cur_nsoft == resource.RLIM_INFINITY else min(cur_nsoft, limits.nofile)
                new_nhard = limits.nofile * 2 if cur_nhard == resource.RLIM_INFINITY else min(cur_nhard, limits.nofile * 2)
                new_nhard = max(new_nhard, new_nsoft)
                resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_NOFILE, (new_nsoft, new_nhard))
            except Exception:
                pass

        return _preexec

    def build_bwrap_command(
        self,
        inner_cmd: List[str],
        cwd_path: Path,
        resource_limits: Optional[ResourceLimits] = None,
    ) -> List[str]:
        """Wrap command with bubblewrap namespaces and minimized filesystem mounts."""
        temp_quota = resource_limits.temp_storage_quota_bytes if resource_limits else (40 * 1024 * 1024)
        bwrap = [
            self.bwrap_path or "bwrap",
            "--ro-bind", "/usr", "/usr",
            "--ro-bind", "/lib", "/lib",
            "--ro-bind", "/bin", "/bin",
            "--proc", "/proc",
            "--dev", "/dev",
            "--new-session",
            "--die-with-parent",
            "--cap-drop", "ALL",
            "--unshare-all",
            "--unshare-net",
        ]

        if os.path.exists("/lib64"):
            bwrap.extend(["--ro-bind", "/lib64", "/lib64"])

        # Minimal /etc bindings for dynamic linkers and user resolution
        for etc_f in ("/etc/ld.so.cache", "/etc/ld.so.conf", "/etc/ld.so.conf.d", "/etc/alternatives", "/etc/passwd"):
            if os.path.exists(etc_f):
                bwrap.extend(["--ro-bind", etc_f, etc_f])

        # tmpfs /tmp with explicit temp_storage_quota_bytes (--size MUST precede --tmpfs)
        bwrap.extend(["--size", str(temp_quota), "--tmpfs", "/tmp"])

        # Domain bindings
        bwrap.extend([
            "--ro-bind", str(self.path_resolver.input_root), str(self.path_resolver.input_root),
            "--bind", str(self.path_resolver.forensic_root), str(self.path_resolver.forensic_root),
            "--chdir", str(cwd_path),
        ])

        if os.path.exists("/opt/kavach/runtime"):
            bwrap.extend(["--ro-bind", "/opt/kavach/runtime", "/opt/kavach/runtime"])
        elif os.path.exists("/opt/kavach"):
            bwrap.extend(["--ro-bind", "/opt/kavach", "/opt/kavach"])

        bwrap.append("--")
        bwrap.extend(inner_cmd)
        return bwrap

    async def _execute_subprocess(
        self,
        argv: List[str],
        cwd_path: Path,
        cwd_uri: str,
        env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        timeout: Optional[float] = None,
        isolation_profile: Optional[IsolationProfile] = None,
        resource_class: Optional[ResourceClass] = None,
        input_bytes: Optional[bytes] = None,
        requested_tool: str = "",
        resolved_path: Optional[str] = None,
        executable_sha256: Optional[str] = None,
        network_policy: str = "NETWORK_NONE",
    ) -> ExecutionResult:
        """Unified internal runner for out-of-process subprocess execution."""
        start_time = time.time()
        exec_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        timeout_sec = min(timeout or self.default_timeout, self.policy.max_timeout_seconds)
        res_class = resource_class or ResourceClass.STANDARD
        limits = DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES.get(res_class, DEFAULT_RESOURCE_PROFILES[ResourceClass.STANDARD])

        profile = self.policy.decide_isolation_profile(isolation_profile, self.bwrap_available)
        clean_env = self.build_sanitized_environment(env)

        if profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED and self.bwrap_available:
            final_cmd = self.build_bwrap_command(argv, cwd_path, limits)
        else:
            final_cmd = argv

        # Setup Disk Streaming Sinks with explicit byte quotas
        stdout_art_id = f"tool_stdout_{exec_id[:8]}"
        stderr_art_id = f"tool_stderr_{exec_id[:8]}"
        stdout_path = self.path_resolver.artifact_root / f"{stdout_art_id}.txt"
        stderr_path = self.path_resolver.artifact_root / f"{stderr_art_id}.txt"
        stdout_sink = StreamSink(
            stdout_path,
            max_bytes=limits.stdout_quota_bytes,
            quota_reason=QuotaExceededReason.STDOUT_QUOTA_EXCEEDED,
        )
        stderr_sink = StreamSink(
            stderr_path,
            max_bytes=limits.stderr_quota_bytes,
            quota_reason=QuotaExceededReason.STDERR_QUOTA_EXCEEDED,
        )

        # Spawn Subprocess with Dynamic Resource Limits
        preexec = self._build_rlimit_preexec(timeout_sec, res_class)
        logger.info(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Spawning ({profile.value}, {res_class.value}): {' '.join(argv[:6])}")

        proc = None
        timed_out = False
        exit_code = -1
        quota_breached = False
        quota_reason: Optional[QuotaExceededReason] = None

        try:
            proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
                final_cmd[0],
                *final_cmd[1:],
                cwd=str(cwd_path),
                env=clean_env,
                stdin=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE if input_bytes else None,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                start_new_session=True,
                preexec_fn=preexec,
            )

            if input_bytes and proc.stdin:
                try:
                    proc.stdin.write(input_bytes)
                    await proc.stdin.drain()
                    proc.stdin.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass

            async def _drain_sink(stream: Optional[asyncio.StreamReader], sink: StreamSink):
                nonlocal quota_breached, quota_reason
                if not stream:
                    return
                try:
                    while True:
                        chunk = await stream.read(8192)
                        if not chunk:
                            break
                        sink.write(chunk)
                except StreamQuotaExceededError as qe:
                    quota_breached = True
                    quota_reason = qe.reason
                    logger.warning(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Stream quota exceeded ({qe.reason.value}). Terminating process group.")
                    if proc and proc.pid:
                        self._kill_process_group(proc.pid)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                finally:
                    sink.close()

            stdout_drain_task = asyncio.create_task(_drain_sink(proc.stdout, stdout_sink))
            stderr_drain_task = asyncio.create_task(_drain_sink(proc.stderr, stderr_sink))

            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(proc.wait(), timeout=timeout_sec)
                exit_code = proc.returncode if proc.returncode is not None else 0
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                timed_out = True
                logger.warning(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Timeout after {timeout_sec}s. Terminating process group.")
                self._kill_process_group(proc.pid)
                try:
                    await asyncio.wait_for(proc.wait(), timeout=1.0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                exit_code = -9

            try:
                await asyncio.wait_for(
                    asyncio.gather(stdout_drain_task, stderr_drain_task, return_exceptions=True),
                    timeout=2.0,
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            finally:
                stdout_sink.close()
                stderr_sink.close()

            if proc and proc.returncode in (-25, -signal.SIGXFSZ, 153):
                quota_breached = True
                quota_reason = QuotaExceededReason.FILE_SIZE_LIMIT

        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[EXEC-{exec_id[:8]}] Subprocess execution failed: {e}")
            stdout_sink.close()
            stderr_sink.close()
            if proc and proc.pid:
                self._kill_process_group(proc.pid)
            return ExecutionResult(
                execution_id=exec_id,
                status="failed",
                exit_code=-1,
                stderr=str(e),
                duration_ms=int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000),
                working_directory=cwd_uri,
                isolation_profile=isolation_profile or IsolationProfile.HARDENED,
                effective_isolation=profile,
                requested_tool=requested_tool,
                resolved_executable_path=resolved_path,
                executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
                requested_network_policy=network_policy,
                effective_network_policy="NETWORK_NONE" if profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED else "NETWORK_ALLOWED",
                resource_class=res_class.value,
                effective_resource_limits=limits.to_dict(),
                backend="bwrap_local" if profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED else "local_process",
                warnings=[str(e)],
                quota_exceeded=quota_breached,
                quota_reason=quota_reason.value if quota_reason else None,
            )

        duration_ms = int((time.time() - start_time) * 1000)

        if stdout_sink.total_bytes > 0:
            self.path_resolver.register_artifact(stdout_art_id, stdout_path)
        if stderr_sink.total_bytes > 0:
            self.path_resolver.register_artifact(stderr_art_id, stderr_path)

        out_summary, out_preview, out_trunc = AdaptiveOutputCompactor.compact_sink(
            stdout_sink, "stdout", stdout_art_id
        )
        err_summary, err_preview, err_trunc = AdaptiveOutputCompactor.compact_sink(
            stderr_sink, "stderr", stderr_art_id
        )

        status = "timeout" if timed_out else ("failed" if quota_breached else ("completed" if exit_code == 0 else "failed"))
        warnings = []
        if quota_breached:
            warnings.append(f"Execution quota exceeded: {quota_reason.value if quota_reason else 'QUOTA_EXCEEDED'}")
        if timed_out:
            warnings.append(f"Command execution timed out after {timeout_sec} seconds")
        if exit_code != 0 and not timed_out and not quota_breached:
            warnings.append(f"Command returned non-zero exit code: {exit_code}")

        return ExecutionResult(
            execution_id=exec_id,
            status=status,
            exit_code=exit_code,
            stdout=out_summary,
            stderr=err_summary,
            stdout_head=stdout_sink.head_str,
            stdout_tail=stdout_sink.tail_str,
            stderr_head=stderr_sink.head_str,
            stderr_tail=stderr_sink.tail_str,
            stdout_bytes=stdout_sink.total_bytes,
            stderr_bytes=stderr_sink.total_bytes,
            stdout_artifact_id=stdout_art_id if stdout_sink.total_bytes > 0 else None,
            stderr_artifact_id=stderr_art_id if stderr_sink.total_bytes > 0 else None,
            stdout_preview=out_preview,
            stderr_preview=err_preview,
            full_output_artifact_id=stdout_art_id if stdout_sink.total_bytes > 0 else (stderr_art_id if stderr_sink.total_bytes > 0 else None),
            duration_ms=duration_ms,
            working_directory=cwd_uri,
            isolation_profile=isolation_profile or IsolationProfile.HARDENED,
            effective_isolation=profile,
            requested_tool=requested_tool,
            resolved_executable_path=resolved_path,
            executable_sha256=executable_sha256,
            requested_network_policy=network_policy,
            effective_network_policy="NETWORK_NONE" if profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED else "NETWORK_ALLOWED",
            resource_class=res_class.value,
            effective_resource_limits=limits.to_dict(),
            backend="bwrap_local" if profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED else "local_process",
            warnings=warnings,
            truncated=(out_trunc or err_trunc or quota_breached),
            quota_exceeded=quota_breached,
            quota_reason=quota_reason.value if quota_reason else None,
        )

    async def run_command(

    ) -> ExecutionResult:
        """
        Execute an allowlisted forensic CLI command out-of-process.
        Resolves logical tool name through ToolExecutionSpec and validates arguments.
        """
        spec, validated_argv = self.policy.resolve_and_validate_tool(cmd)

--- [Step 51 | THINKING] ---
I'm examining the sandbox setup—it isolates namespaces and binds the system directories read-only while mounting the forensic root as read-write, but I notice the venv and site-packages paths referenced via PYTHONPATH point into the forensic workspace itself, which could be a gap in the isolation.

I'm also wondering if jadx is reachable inside bwrap depending on where KAVACH_JADX_PATH points, since paths outside the bound directories would fail; meanwhile there's no seccomp filter, and I'm checking whether RLIMIT_AS would interfere with JVM startup given jadx's memory needs, plus whether RLIMIT_NPROC behaves correctly across the new user namespace.

Then I'm flagging that using preexec_fn for forking bwrap while asyncio.to_thread runs elsewhere is risky since Python explicitly warns against preexec_fn in multi-threaded processes — a possible deadlock worth noting as medium risk. I'm also confirming the timeout/kill logic works correctly given `--new-session` and `--die-with-parent` semantics, and noting that stdout artifacts get registered for every execution via the artifact root.

But I realize these stdout/stderr "artifacts" actually go through path_resolver.register_artifact, not ArtifactStore — meaning there are two separate artifact registries, and the full tool output referenced by `full_output_artifact_id` in ExecutionResult never actually lands in the content-addressable store or ledger. That's a real provenance gap.

I also note decide_isolation_profile fails closed correctly when bwrap is missing, raising a policy violation rather than silently falling back — good. But BwrapBackend.is_available always returns True even when bwrap isn't present, so a FAILED result can still misreport "HIGH" isolation, which is a minor inconsistency. Now I want to check whether jadx resolution via try_resolve_tool could return a host path outside the bind mounts, which would break the sandboxing guarantees when run_command executes it.

    async def run_python(
        self,
        code: str,
        cwd_uri: str = "forensic://work",
        env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None,
        timeout: Optional[float] = None,
        isolation_profile: Optional[IsolationProfile] = None,
        resource_class: Optional[ResourceClass] = None,
        script_name: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> ExecutionResult:
        """
        Execute a Python code string out-of-process in a subprocess.
        Writes script to FORENSIC_WORKSPACE/.scripts/, parses syntax pre-exec,
        and collects structured results and produced artifacts.
        """
        self.policy.validate_python_code(code)

        preamble = (
            "import os, builtins, io\n"
            "def _resolve_fn(f, w):\n"
            "    s = str(f)\n"
            "    if s.startswith('input://'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_INPUT_ROOT', '.'), s[8:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('input:/'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_INPUT_ROOT', '.'), s[7:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('forensic://'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_FORENSIC_ROOT', '.'), s[11:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('forensic:/'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_FORENSIC_ROOT', '.'), s[10:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('repair://'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_REPAIR_ROOT', '.'), s[9:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('repair:/'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_REPAIR_ROOT', '.'), s[8:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('artifact://'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_ARTIFACT_ROOT', '.'), s[11:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    elif s.startswith('artifact:/'):\n"
            "        return os.path.join(os.environ.get('KAVACH_ARTIFACT_ROOT', '.'), s[10:].lstrip('/'))\n"
            "    return f\n"
            "_orig_open = builtins.open\n"
            "_orig_io_open = io.open\n"
            "def _kavach_open(file, *args, **kwargs):\n"
            "    s = str(file)\n"
            "    if '://' in s or ':/' in s or s.startswith(('input/', 'forensic/', 'repair/')):\n"
            "        mode = args[0] if args else kwargs.get('mode', 'r')\n"
            "        for_write = any(m in mode for m in ('w', 'a', '+', 'x'))\n"
            "        file = _resolve_fn(file, for_write)\n"
            "    return _orig_io_open(file, *args, **kwargs)\n"
            "builtins.open = _kavach_open\n"
            "io.open = _kavach_open\n\n"
        )
        final_code = preamble + code

        # Write script to forensic workspace scripts folder
        s_name = script_name or f"workbench_script_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.py"
        script_uri = f"forensic://.scripts/{s_name}"
        script_path = self.path_resolver.write_text(script_uri, final_code, overwrite=True)

        cwd_path = self.path_resolver.resolve_path(cwd_uri, for_write=True)
        cwd_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        target_py = self.python_bin
        effective_profile = self.policy.decide_isolation_profile(isolation_profile, self.bwrap_available)
        if effective_profile == IsolationProfile.HARDENED and self.bwrap_available:
            if not target_py.startswith(("/usr", "/bin", "/opt/kavach")):
                target_py = "/usr/bin/python3" if os.path.exists("/usr/bin/python3") else "/bin/python3"

        py_argv = [target_py, str(script_path)]
        return await self._execute_subprocess(
            argv=py_argv,
            cwd_path=cwd_path,
            cwd_uri=cwd_uri,
            env=env,
            timeout=timeout,
            isolation_profile=isolation_profile,
            resource_class=resource_class or ResourceClass.STANDARD,
            requested_tool="python3",
            resolved_path=target_py,
            network_policy="NETWORK_NONE",
        )

    def _kill_process_group(self, pid: int) -> None:
        """Reliably terminate a process group (tree) with SIGTERM then SIGKILL."""
        try:
            pgid = os.getpgid(pid)
            os.killpg(pgid, signal.SIGTERM)
            time.sleep(0.2)
            os.killpg(pgid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
            try:
                os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
            except Exception:
                pass
        except Exception as e:
            logger.debug(f"Error killing process group {pid}: {e}")

# 5. Workbench Policy Engine
# ============================================================================

class WorkbenchPolicy:
    """
    Evaluates permissions for python execution, shell command execution,
    filesystem operations, and isolation profile selection.
    """

    # Forensic allowed CLIs - strictly excludes python and python3
    DEFAULT_ALLOWED_FORENSIC_TOOLS: Set[str] = {
        "file",
        "strings",
        "readelf",
        "nm",
        "objdump",
        "unzip",
        "zip",
        "zipalign",
        "apksigner",
        "jadx",
        "apktool",
        "rizin",
        "rz-bin",
        "rz-ax",
        "capa",
        "floss",
        "yara",
        "yara-x",
        "diff",
        "patch",
        "git",
        "pytest",
    }

    DISALLOWED_BINARIES: Set[str] = {
        "python",
        "python3",
        "rm",
        "sudo",
        "su",
        "bash",
        "sh",
        "zsh",
        "dash",
        "nc",
        "ncat",
        "netcat",
        "curl",
        "wget",
        "ssh",
        "scp",
        "dd",
        "mkfs",
        "chmod",
        "chown",
        "kill",
        "pkill",
        "killall",
    }

    DISALLOWED_SHELL_METACHARS: Set[str] = {";", "&", "|", "`", "$", "<", ">", "\n", "\r"}

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed_binaries: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
        network_enabled: bool = False,
        allow_normal_profile: bool = False,
        max_timeout_seconds: float = 300.0,
        default_timeout_seconds: float = 30.0,
        max_output_bytes: int = 10 * 1024 * 1024,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed_binaries = set(allowed_binaries or self.DEFAULT_ALLOWED_FORENSIC_TOOLS)
        # Ensure python and python3 are NEVER in allowed_binaries for shell_exec
        self.allowed_binaries.discard("python")
        self.allowed_binaries.discard("python3")

        self.network_enabled = network_enabled
        self.allow_normal_profile = allow_normal_profile
        self.max_timeout_seconds = max_timeout_seconds
        self.default_timeout_seconds = default_timeout_seconds
        self.max_output_bytes = max_output_bytes

        # Discover and register ToolExecutionSpecs
        self._tool_specs: dict[str, ToolExecutionSpec] = {}
        self._initialize_tool_specs()
        self.tool_specs: Mapping[str, ToolExecutionSpec] = types.MappingProxyType(self._tool_specs)

        # Cache bubblewrap capability probe
        self.bwrap_capability = probe_bwrap_capability()

    def register_tool_spec(self, spec: ToolExecutionSpec) -> None:
        """Register or update a ToolExecutionSpec in the policy registry."""
        if not is_inside_approved_immutable_root(spec.canonical_path):
            raise WorkbenchPolicyViolation(
                f"Tool canonical path '{spec.canonical_path}' is outside approved immutable roots."
            )
        self._tool_specs[spec.logical_name] = spec

    def _initialize_tool_specs(self) -> None:
        """Register ToolExecutionSpecs for all allowed forensic tools with canonical paths and validator IDs."""
        validator_map: dict[str, str] = {
            "file": "file_args_v1",
            "strings": "strings_args_v1",
            "readelf": "readelf_args_v1",
            "nm": "nm_args_v1",
            "objdump": "objdump_args_v1",
            "unzip": "unzip_args_v1",
            "zip": "zip_args_v1",
            "zipalign": "zipalign_args_v1",
            "apksigner": "apksigner_args_v1",
            "jadx": "jadx_args_v1",
            "apktool": "apktool_args_v1",
            "rizin": "rizin_args_v1",
            "rz-bin": "readelf_args_v1",
            "rz-ax": "strings_args_v1",
            "capa": "capa_args_v1",
            "floss": "floss_args_v1",
            "yara": "yara_args_v1",
            "yara-x": "yara_args_v1",
            "diff": "diff_args_v1",
            "patch": "patch_args_v1",
            "git": "git_args_v1",
            "pytest": "pytest_args_v1",
        }

        for tool_name in self.allowed_binaries:
            val_id = validator_map.get(tool_name, "passthrough_v1")
            canonical_path: Optional[str] = None
            resolved_realpath: Optional[str] = None

            for root in APPROVED_IMMUTABLE_ROOTS:
                cand = os.path.join(root, tool_name)
                if os.path.exists(cand) and os.access(cand, os.X_OK):
                    canonical_path = cand
                    resolved_realpath = os.path.realpath(cand)
    """Raised when an operation or tool invocation violates workbench security policy."""
    pass


# Approved immutable installation roots for trusted executables
APPROVED_IMMUTABLE_ROOTS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "/opt/kavach/runtime/bin",
    "/opt/kavach/bin",
    "/usr/bin",
    "/bin",
    "/usr/local/bin",
)


def is_inside_approved_immutable_root(candidate_path: str) -> bool:
    """Strict path boundary containment check ensuring candidate is inside an approved immutable root."""
    try:
        cand_p = Path(os.path.realpath(candidate_path)).resolve()
        for root in APPROVED_IMMUTABLE_ROOTS:
            root_p = Path(os.path.realpath(root)).resolve()
            if cand_p == root_p or cand_p.is_relative_to(root_p):
                return True
        return False
    except Exception:
        return False

I'm spotting a mismatch: demo_tools_available() offers run_jadx whenever try_resolve_tool finds jadx anywhere on PATH, but the workbench policy only permits jadx from specific immutable root directories. The jadx launcher itself is just a shell script with a shebang that calls java, which is fine since it's an interpreted script rather than a disallowed binary. Now I want to verify the broader environment — Python version, Postgres, jadx, bwrap, llama-server, and the model itself.

#!/bin/bash
# KAVACH AI 2.0 backend verification gate.
#
# Required suites (UNIT, CONTRACT, SECURITY, INTEGRATION) must pass with zero skips:
# a skipped security or integration test is reported as a failure, never as a pass.
# Optional suites run only when their private inputs are provisioned and are otherwise
# reported as NOT RUN:
#   ACCEPTANCE  KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES=<dir of private APKs>
#   MODEL       KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE=1 plus KAVACH_LLAMA_SERVER_PATH / KAVACH_MODEL_PATH (and optional GPU)

set -o pipefail

PROJECT_ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || (cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd))"
cd "${PROJECT_ROOT}/backend" || exit 1

if [ -x ".venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON=".venv/bin/python"
elif [ -x "venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON="venv/bin/python"
else
    PYTHON="python3"
fi

UNIT="test_agent_regression.py test_investigation_service.py test_ledger.py test_investigation_ledger.py test_tool_observation_boundary.py test_frontier_and_multitool.py test_investigation_loop.py test_hypothesis_adjudication.py test_model_gateway.py test_reasoning_provider.py test_risk_and_reporting.py test_system_doctor_governor.py test_runtime_toolchain.py test_orchestrator.py test_native_tool_compatibility_probe.py"
CONTRACT="test_tool_contracts.py test_artifact_store.py"
SECURITY="test_execution_broker.py test_forensic_execution_broker.py test_workbench_sandbox.py test_workbench_security_hardening.py test_filesystem_isolation.py"
INTEGRATION="test_persistence_lifecycle.py test_service_postgres.py test_environment_portability.py"
REPAIR="test_git_repair.py"
ACCEPTANCE="test_live_analyzers.py test_orchestrator.py test_tool_contracts.py test_reconstruction_engine.py test_golden_benchmarks.py"
MODEL="test_live_model_smoke.py test_golden_a_live.py test_golden_b_live.py test_workbench_live_qwen.py test_native_tool_compatibility_probe.py"

LOG_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "${LOG_DIR}"' EXIT
declare -a REPORT
FAILED=0
NOT_RUN=0

run_suite() {
    local name="$1" marker="$2" files="$3"
    local log="${LOG_DIR}/${name}.log"
    echo "=============================================="
    echo "${name}: ${PYTHON} -m pytest -m \"${marker}\""
    echo "=============================================="
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    "${PYTHON}" -m pytest -p no:cacheprovider -q -rs -m "${marker}" ${files} 2>&1 | tee "${log}"
    local rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
    local summary skipped
    summary="$(grep -E '^(=+ )?[0-9]+ (passed|failed|error|skipped|deselected)|no tests ran' "${log}" | tail -1 | sed 's/=//g; s/^ *//; s/ *$//')"
    skipped="$(echo "${summary}" | grep -oE '[0-9]+ skipped' | grep -oE '[0-9]+' || true)"
    if [ "${rc}" -ne 0 ]; then
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s FAIL (%s)' "${name}:" "${summary:-exit ${rc}}")")
        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))
    elif [ -n "${skipped}" ] && [ "${skipped}" -gt 0 ]; then
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s FAIL (%s; skipped tests are not passes)' "${name}:" "${summary}")")
        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))
    else
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s PASS (%s)' "${name}:" "${summary}")")
    fi
}

not_run() {
    REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s NOT RUN (%s)' "$1:" "$2")")
    NOT_RUN=$((NOT_RUN + 1))
}

DEFAULT_MARKER="not acceptance and not model"
run_suite UNIT "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${UNIT}"
run_suite CONTRACT "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${CONTRACT}"
run_suite SECURITY "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${SECURITY}"
run_suite INTEGRATION "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${INTEGRATION}"
run_suite REPAIR "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and repair" "${REPAIR}"

if [ -n "${KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES:-}" ]; then
    run_suite ACCEPTANCE "acceptance and not model" "${ACCEPTANCE}"
else
    not_run ACCEPTANCE "requires KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES"
fi

if [ "${KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE:-0}" = "1" ]; then
    run_suite MODEL "model" "${MODEL}"
else
    not_run MODEL "requires KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE=1 and a local llama-server + GGUF model"
fi

echo "=============================================="
echo "Verification Summary"
echo "=============================================="
for line in "${REPORT[@]}"; do
    echo "${line}"
done
echo "----------------------------------------------"

if [ "${FAILED}" -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "Verdict: FAILED (${FAILED} suite(s) failed)"
    exit 1
fi
if [ "${NOT_RUN}" -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "Verdict: REQUIRED SUITES PASSED; ${NOT_RUN} optional suite(s) NOT RUN"
else
    echo "Verdict: ALL SUITES PASSED"
fi
exit 0

    72	    Handles tool calls, structured JSON validation, and error recovery.
    73	    """

    81	        api_key: str = "local-no-key",
    82	        legacy_text_tool_calls: bool = False,
    83	        transport: Optional[httpx.AsyncBaseTransport] = None,
    84	    ) -> None:
    85	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
    86	        self.timeout = timeout
    87	        self.api_key = api_key
    88	        # Native protocol only by default: tool calls arrive in message.tool_calls. Parsing tool calls
    89	        # out of message text is a legacy compatibility mode that must be requested explicitly.
    90	        self.legacy_text_tool_calls = legacy_text_tool_calls
